<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47939579" data-id="47939579" data-tags="Open Source|Career|Programming" data-score="3448" data-comments="1029" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579" data-time="1777405492">
<a class="thumb" href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/ghostty-leaving-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=mitchellh.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>mitchellh.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mitchellh.com/writing/ghostty-leaving-github" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub</span></a><span class="dom">mitchellh.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting. The move prompts reflection on dependencies, portability, and how maintainers plan long-term collaboration without GitHub’s ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。此举引发对依赖关系、可移植性以及维护者在无 GitHub 生态的情况下如何长期协作的思考。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Career' style='--c:#2dd4bf'>Career</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>2.2</span><span class='m ic-up'>3448</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1029</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47865868" data-id="47865868" data-tags="Startups|Business" data-score="2250" data-comments="761" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868" data-time="1776875365">
<a class="thumb" href="https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#fb7185' data-tag='Startups'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=wheelfront.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>wheelfront.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alberta startup sells no-tech tractors for half price</span><span class='zh'>阿尔伯塔初创以半价销售无科技拖拉机</span></a><span class="dom">wheelfront.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability.</span><span class='zh'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability. By removing electronics, they cut upfront costs and maintenance headaches, challenging mainstream ag-tech pricing and support models.</span><span class='zh'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。去除电子系统后，制造与维护成本下降，这一做法可能影响主流农业科技的定价和售后模式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>2250</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>761</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47687273" data-id="47687273" data-tags="Programming" data-score="2244" data-comments="477" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273" data-time="1775638422">
<a class="thumb" href="https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='piechowski.io' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Git commands I run before reading any code</span><span class='zh'>在读代码前我会执行的 Git 命令</span></a><span class="dom">piechowski.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots.</span><span class='zh'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots. The routine surfaces remotes, recent commits, file changes, and likely entry points, helping you jump straight to meaningful parts.</span><span class='zh'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。这个流程能快速显示远端、最近提交、改动文件以及潜在入口点，帮助开发者直接进入重点代码。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">04</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>John Ternus to become Apple CEO</span><span class='zh'>约翰·特诺斯将任苹果公司 CEO</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession. Ternus, a veteran hardware and product operations leader, would steer Apple’s product roadmap and hardware/software integration in the next era. The move underscores Apple’s emphasis on continuity and a hardware-centric strategy.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。特诺斯长期担任硬件/产品运营负责人，接任后将引领苹果的产品路线与硬件/软件协同。此举突显苹果在保持连续性和以硬件驱动战略方面的长期规划。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://zed.dev/blog/zed-1-0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">zed.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0 marks the first stable release of the Zed language, accompanied by its tooling and documentation.</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 标志着 Zed 语言的首个稳定版本及其工具链的发布。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zed 1.0 marks the first stable release of the Zed language, accompanied by its tooling and documentation. The release signals readiness for broader adoption in systems programming and showcases a focus on safe, expressive syntax and productive tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Zed 1.0 标志着 Zed 语言的首个稳定版本及其工具链的发布。此举表明该语言准备迎接更广泛的应用，强调安全、可读性强的语法和高效的开发工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.newyorker.com/magazine/2026/04/13/sam-altman-may-control-our-future-can-he-be-trusted" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sam Altman may control our future – can he be trusted?</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼可能掌控我们的未来——能被信任吗？</span></a><span class="dom">newyorker.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership.</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼在AI领域的影响力引发关于科技领袖权力与责任平衡的讨论。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership. The piece challenges readers to weigh optimism about rapid innovation against safeguards for society, privacy, and equity. Understanding who shapes AI&#x27;s trajectory matters for engineers and policymakers alike.</span><span class='zh'>萨姆·奥特曼在AI领域的影响力引发关于科技领袖权力与责任平衡的讨论。文章促使读者权衡对快速创新的乐观态度与对社会、隐私和公平的保障之间的取舍。理解谁在塑造AI的未来，对工程师与政策制定者都至关重要。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.stepsecurity.io/blog/axios-compromised-on-npm-malicious-versions-drop-remote-access-trojan" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Axios compromised on NPM – Malicious versions drop remote access trojan</span><span class='zh'>Axios 在 NPM 上被劫持：恶意版本投放远程访问木马</span></a><span class="dom">stepsecurity.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions. The attack highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk for widely used libraries, and operators should audit dependencies, pin safe versions, and monitor for unusual network activity after updates.</span><span class='zh'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。此类供应链攻击再次暴露了对广泛使用库的依赖风险，建议锁定版本、进行依赖审计，并在更新后监测异常网络活动。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bryankeller.github.io/2026/04/08/porting-mac-os-x-nintendo-wii.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I ported Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii</span><span class='zh'>我把 Mac OS X 移植到了任天堂 Wii</span></a><span class="dom">bryankeller.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability.</span><span class='zh'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability. The write-up highlights bootstrapping, driver gaps, and the kinds of trade-offs required when attempting to run a modern OS on a legacy console.</span><span class='zh'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。文章聚焦引导启动、驱动缺口，以及在旧世代主机上运行现代操作系统所需的取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/google-broke-its-promise-me-now-ice-has-my-data" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google broke its promise to me – now ICE has my data</span><span class='zh'>谷歌违背承诺，现在 ICE 已掌握我的数据</span></a><span class="dom">eff.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy. The case underscores the tension between platform commitments and law enforcement data demands, and the need for stronger privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。此事凸显平台承诺与执法需求之间的矛盾，强调需要更强的隐私控制与透明度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/glasswing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Project Glasswing: Securing critical software for the AI era</span><span class='zh'>玻璃翼计划：为AI时代的关键软件提供安全防护</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections. The initiative signals a shift toward AI-era security by treating software as an active, risk-prone surface rather than a static asset.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。该举措标志着安全从静态资产转向对AI驱动环境中高风险表面的主动防护。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The EU will require replaceable batteries in all phones starting 2027, aiming to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste. The rule could force redesigns, disrupt supply chains, and influence consumer costs.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟将于2027年要求所有手机具备可更换电池，旨在延长设备寿命并减少电子垃圾。此举可能迫使厂商重新设计，影响供应链并提高一些消费者成本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Claude Code Source Leak: fake tools, frustration regexes, undercover mode</span><span class='zh'>Claude 代码源泄露：假工具、挫败感正则、潜伏模式</span></a><span class="dom">alex000kim.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode. The incident highlights the fragility of model provenance and the risk of leaked tooling being manipulated to mislead researchers. The takeaway is a call for stronger provenance controls and safer, auditable tooling around AI code-generation.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。此事凸显了模型来源的脆弱性，以及泄露工具被操纵以误导研究者的风险。核心启示是需要加强来源追溯与对 AI 代码生成工具的安全、可审计设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users. This signals growing demand for fine-grained, user-friendly network policies on desktop Linux and could influence future firewall tooling and UX.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。这类细分的网络策略工具日益成为需求，可能推动未来防火墙工具的用户体验与实现方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/42796" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Issue: Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks with Feb updates</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在二月更新后难以处理复杂工程任务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability. The note highlights current limits of AI coding assistants for serious production work and the need for ongoing improvements.</span><span class='zh'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。该评测强调了 AI 编码助手在正式生产场景中的局限性，以及持续改进的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.cbsnews.com/live-updates/artemis-ii-splashdown-return/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II safely splashes down</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号安全落水回归</span></a><span class="dom">cbsnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration. The return validates deep-space readiness and endurance in human spaceflight, setting the stage for subsequent lunar missions and partnerships with industry.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。此次归来验证了深空任务的可行性与人类航天的耐久性，并为后续任务及产业合作奠定基础。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aisle.com/blog/ai-cybersecurity-after-mythos-the-jagged-frontier" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Small models also found the vulnerabilities that Mythos found</span><span class='zh'>小模型也发现了 Mythos 发现的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">aisle.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security. This broadens risk assessment to include a wider range of models and prompts tighter evaluation and defense strategies.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。这使安全评估需覆盖更广的模型范围，并推动更严格的测试与防护措施。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HERMES.md in commit messages causes requests to route to extra usage billing</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中的 HERMES.md 会将请求路由至额外的使用量计费</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md.</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md. The quirk demonstrates how tiny pieces of metadata in version control can unexpectedly affect costs, and suggests teams sanitize commit messages or adjust policy to avoid surprise bills.</span><span class='zh'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。为避免意外账单，团队应对提交信息进行清洗并审查计费策略。</span></p>

The piece reframes Azure’s evolution as a case study in cloud trust and product governance.</span><span class='zh'>一位前 Azure Core 工程师揭示削弱用户信任的决策，涉及功能弃用、更新不透明与治理摩擦等问题，令开发者和管理员感到挫败。该分析将 Azure 的演变视为云信任与产品治理的案例研究。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic unveils Claude Design, a design-focused variant of its AI assistant intended to accelerate creative workflows for designers and product teams. The release signals AI tools maturing into specialist roles within design and product development.</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 推出 Claude Design，这是一个面向设计任务的 Claude 变体，旨在加速设计师和产品团队的创意工作流程。它将 Claude 的推理能力用于头脑风暴、原型设计和视觉生成，并具备协作功能与安全保护。该发布表明 AI 工具正在向设计和产品开发的专业化角色演进。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://anchor.host/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/wordpress-plugin-supply-chain-attack-1.webp' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='anchor.host' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">34</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Someone bought 30 WordPress plugins and planted a backdoor in all of them</span><span class='zh'>有人买下 30 个 WordPress 插件并在全部插件中植入后门</span></a><span class="dom">anchor.host</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one. The stunt highlights how third-party extensions can compromise many sites if integrity checks and monitoring are lax.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。这暴露了第三方插件的供应链和信任机制漏洞，强调加强插件审计、完整性校验和运行时监控的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Western economies have lost touch with making things, and now their coding skills are at risk as software becomes more opaque. <p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Docker pull failure in Spain is linked to Cloudflare blocking football-related requests, illustrating how regional filtering can disrupt developer workflows. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code Unpacked offers a visual tour of Claude&#x27;s coding workflows, showing how to structure prompts and interpret results with diagrams and examples.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code Unpacked 展示了 Claude 在编码任务中的工作流，通过图解和示例讲解提示设计与结果解读。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code Unpacked offers a visual tour of Claude&#x27;s coding workflows, showing how to structure prompts and interpret results with diagrams and examples. It helps developers understand when to rely on AI for coding tasks versus traditional tooling and highlights its limitations.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code Unpacked 展示了 Claude 在编码任务中的工作流，通过图解和示例讲解提示设计与结果解读。强调在实际开发中何时依赖 AI 辅助、何时回归传统工具，以及其潜在局限。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ce8jzr423p9o" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Artemis II crew take “spectacular” image of Earth</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组拍出壮观的地球照片</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Artemis II crew captured a spectacular image of Earth from near-space, showcasing our planet in a breathtaking vantage point during the mission phase.</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组在近空间阶段拍摄出地球的壮观影像，从独特视角展现了这颗行星的美丽与脆弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Artemis II crew captured a spectacular image of Earth from near-space, showcasing our planet in a breathtaking vantage point during the mission phase. The shot underscores NASA&#x27;s focus on crewed lunar exploration and the growing role of high-quality space photography in public engagement. It also highlights the technical readiness required for long-duration deep-space missions.</span><span class='zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组在近空间阶段拍摄出地球的壮观影像，从独特视角展现了这颗行星的美丽与脆弱。这张照片体现了 NASA 对载人月球探索的持续推进，以及高质量太空影像在公众参与中的作用，同时也凸显了支撑长期深空任务的技术准备。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/introducing-chatgpt-images-2-0/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>ChatGPT Images 2.0</span><span class='zh'>ChatGPT 图像 2.0</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats. It promises higher fidelity visuals, more robust prompting controls, and faster rendering, making it easier for creators and developers to prototype visual AI tools directly in conversation.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。该更新带来更高保真度的图像、更强的提示控制和更快的渲染速度，为创作者和开发者在对话中原生原型化视觉 AI 工具提供新途径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://openai.com/index/codex-for-almost-everything/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Codex for almost everything</span><span class='zh'>几乎适用于一切场景的 Codex</span></a><span class="dom">openai.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators. The move signals growing demand for single-model copilots that can handle coding, data tasks, automation, and workflow integration, reducing context-switching.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。此举反映出市场对单一模型兼顾编码、数据处理、自动化与工作流整合的需求日增，减少上下文切换。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental Linux compatibility layer aims to run Linux userland on Windows 9x-era systems. It demonstrates the ongoing appeal of cross-OS compatibility, even in retro environments, and offers insight into how modern tooling could be adapted for older platforms.</span><span class='zh'>一个实验性 Linux 兼容层尝试在 Windows 95/98 等旧系统上运行 Linux 用户空间。它体现了跨操作系统兼容性的持续吸引力，并为将来在老平台上应用现代工具提供了洞见。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-27/microsoft-to-stop-sharing-revenue-with-main-ai-partner-openai" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI end their exclusive and revenue-sharing deal</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止独家及分成协议</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal.</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal. The move loosens a decade-long alignment that helped accelerate AI adoption in Azure and Copilot, while inviting new partnerships for OpenAI and forcing Microsoft to recalibrate its cloud-AI bets. The change reframes the competitive landscape for AI platforms and developer access.</span><span class='zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。此举让 OpenAI 能与更多伙伴合作，微软也需重新布局云端 AI 战略。此举可能重塑 AI 平台生态的竞争格局与开发者接入格局。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rowan441.github.io/1dchess/chess.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>1D Chess</span><span class='zh'>一维棋</span></a><span class="dom">rowan441.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy.</span><span class='zh'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy. It’s a compact exploration of how constraints alter classical chess dynamics and makes for a playful, AI-friendly coding or game-design exercise.</span><span class='zh'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。也是一个有趣的 AI 研究/游戏设计练习，便于实现与教学。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale. The result highlights progress in efficient, accessible model sizes for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。此成果凸显了在高效、易获得的模型规模上实现进步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nasa.gov/gallery/lunar-flyby/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lunar Flyby</span><span class='zh'>月球飞掠</span></a><span class="dom">nasa.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features.</span><span class='zh'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features. Such flybys refine trajectory design for future missions and provide rich science visuals for public outreach. They also help calibrate navigation systems used across deep-space operations.</span><span class='zh'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。此类飞掠有助于优化未来任务的轨道设计并为公众科普提供直观的科学画面。它们还用于校准深空任务的导航系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.0xsid.com/blog/wont-download-your-app" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I won&#x27;t download your app. The web version is a-ok</span><span class='zh'>我不想下载你的应用，网页版本就够用</span></a><span class="dom">0xsid.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead.</span><span class='zh'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead. Web apps can be quicker to update and lighter on device storage, reducing platform lock-in, though they may trade off certain features like push notifications and offline access.</span><span class='zh'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。网页应用更新更快、占用存储更少，降低了对特定平台的绑定，但在推送通知和离线功能等方面存在权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fingerprint.com/blog/firefox-tor-indexeddb-privacy-vulnerability/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We found a stable Firefox identifier linking all your private Tor identities</span><span class='zh'>发现 Firefox 的稳定标识符可连接你所有的 Tor 身份</span></a><span class="dom">fingerprint.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity. The finding underscores how browser storage features can erode privacy in privacy-focused tooling and calls for mitigations.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。这一发现揭示了浏览器存储机制对隐私工具构成的潜在威胁，并需要相应缓解措施。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations. The post outlines strategies to maintain roughly $10K MRR per product at tiny ongoing costs, highlighting how a low-cost, automated stack can scale.</span><span class='zh'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。作者分享了将每个项目的月耗控在约20美元、个别产品实现约1万美元MRR的做法，挑战了对高成本技术栈的普遍认知。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ergosphere.blog/posts/the-machines-are-fine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The threat is comfortable drift toward not understanding what you&#x27;re doing</span><span class='zh'>威胁来自对所做之事逐渐变得难以理解的舒适漂移</span></a><span class="dom">ergosphere.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing.</span><span class='zh'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing. The piece argues that as tooling grows more capable, critical thinking and verification risk being outsourced to machines. Maintaining explicit mental models and observability is essential to reliability and security.</span><span class='zh'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。文章强调，工具越强大，批判性思维与验证越易外包给机器，需保持可观测性与清晰的认知模型以维护可靠性与安全。</span></p>

It also highlights pragmatic tradeoffs and how to structure AI-assisted workflows.</span><span class='zh'>在八年的野心推动下，开发者用 AI 工具仅用三个月就打造出 Syntaqlite AI。此案例展示了 AI 如何显著加速原型开发和工具链搭建，让长期的梦想在短时间内变为现实。文中也讨论了实际取舍与如何组织 AI 辅助的工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localsend/localsend" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Localsend: An open-source cross-platform alternative to AirDrop</span><span class='zh'>Localsend：开源跨平台的 AirDrop 替代</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic.</span><span class='zh'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic. It emphasizes privacy and platform-agnostic usability for Windows, macOS, and Linux users.</span><span class='zh'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。面向 Windows、macOS、Linux 用户，强调隐私保护与无缝跨平台使用。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.github.com/gh-stack/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Stacked PRs</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 堆叠 PR</span></a><span class="dom">github.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally. The gh-stack tool provides commands to create, rebase, and track the stack, helping reviewers stay in sync across related changes.</span><span class='zh'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。gh-stack 提供创建、变基和跟踪等命令，使评审人能够在相关改动之间保持同步。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://isayeter.com/posts/digitalocean-to-hetzner-migration/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Migrating from DigitalOcean to Hetzner</span><span class='zh'>从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner：零停机与成本下降</span></a><span class="dom">isayeter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings.</span><span class='zh'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings. The piece shares practical steps, from plan and backups to DNS cutover and testing, illustrating how to optimize cloud spend without sacrificing availability.</span><span class='zh'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。文章分享从计划、备份到 DNS 切换与测试的要点，说明在不牺牲可用性的前提下实现云成本优化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Caveman: Why use many token when few token do trick</span><span class='zh'>Caveman：为何用多 Token 不如用少 Token 就能完成任务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume.</span><span class='zh'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume. The project demonstrates token-efficient techniques that reduce compute and cost while preserving performance.</span><span class='zh'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。该项目提供了实现标记效率的具体方法与实践。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。文章强调推进大胆太空任务时安全评估的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</span><span class='zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</span><span class='zh'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://teybannerman.com/strategy/2026/03/31/how-many-microsoft-copilot-are-there.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How many products does Microsoft have named &#x27;Copilot&#x27;?</span><span class='zh'>微软有多少个以 Copilot 命名的产品？</span></a><span class="dom">teybannerman.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft has deployed the Copilot name on a wide range of products, creating branding overlap across consumer and developer tools.</span><span class='zh'>微软将 Copilot 名称用于大量产品，导致品牌混用，令用户对不同产品的功能与定位产生混淆。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft has deployed the Copilot name on a wide range of products, creating branding overlap across consumer and developer tools. This underscores how single-brand naming can complicate product differentiation and user expectations in a sprawling software portfolio.</span><span class='zh'>微软将 Copilot 名称用于大量产品，导致品牌混用，令用户对不同产品的功能与定位产生混淆。这反映出在庞大软件生态中，单一命名策略如何影响产品区分和用户认知。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://awesomeagents.ai/news/github-fake-stars-investigation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub&#x27;s fake star economy</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 的假星星经济</span></a><span class="dom">awesomeagents.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity. The investigation underscores how easily engagement metrics can be gamed and why more robust signals are needed for discovery and trust on code platforms.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。此事凸显互动指标易被操纵的风险，并推动平台加强对发现与信任的更稳健衡量方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/meta-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-ai-training-data-2026-04-21/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta to start capturing employee mouse movements, keystrokes for AI training</span><span class='zh'>Meta 将开始捕获员工鼠标移动与按键用于 AI 训练数据</span></a><span class="dom">reuters.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns. The policy could reshape corporate data practices and worker autonomy across tech companies. It spotlights the tension between improving AI capabilities and protecting user privacy.</span><span class='zh'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。此举可能重塑科技公司层面的数据实践与员工自主权，凸显提升 AI 能力与保护隐私之间的张力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Switzerland has 25 Gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</span><span class='zh'>自由市场的谎言：瑞士为何有25 Gbit互联网，美国却没有</span></a><span class="dom">sschueller.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</span><span class='zh'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</span><span class='zh'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。文章强调监管选择、投资激励与市场结构如何决定一个国家的连通性。</span></p>

The milestone reduces pressure on IPv4, but many operators still wrestle with dual-stack management and security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。这个里程碑减少了对 IPv4 的依赖，但运营商在双栈管理与安全性方面仍面临挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/amateur-armed-with-chatgpt-vibe-maths-a-60-year-old-problem/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Amateur armed with ChatGPT solves an Erdős problem</span><span class='zh'>业余者借助 ChatGPT 解决 Erdős 问题</span></a><span class="dom">scientificamerican.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible.</span><span class='zh'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible. This episode underscores AI&#x27;s potential to assist mathematical thinking while highlighting the need for rigorous verification of AI-generated proofs. It also raises questions about the boundary between exploration and formal proof.</span><span class='zh'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。此事件凸显了人工智能在辅助数学思考方面的潜力，同时也强调对 AI 生成证明进行严格验证的必要性。它还引发了关于探索与正式证明边界的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/github-copilot-is-moving-to-usage-based-billing/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot is moving to usage-based billing</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将改为按使用量计费</span></a><span class="dom">github.blog</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption. GitHub cites fairer pricing linked to value delivered.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。GitHub 表示新定价更公平、与所创造的价值挂钩。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale.</span><span class='zh'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale. The project explores how era-specific design choices impact capabilities and efficiency, offering a playful, instructive counterpoint to conventional LLM development.</span><span class='zh'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。該項目探討不同時代下的設計取捨如何影響模型能力與效率，為傳統大模型開發提供有趣且具啟發性的對照。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.phoronix.com/news/Steam-On-Linux-Tops-5p" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Steam on Linux Use Skyrocketed Above 5% in March</span><span class='zh'>Steam 在 Linux 的使用率于三月突破 5%</span></a><span class="dom">phoronix.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Steam usage on Linux climbed past 5% in March, signaling growing gamer adoption on Linux platforms.</span><span class='zh'>三月 Linux 上 Steam 用户占比超过 5%，显示 Linux 平台的玩家采纳在增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Steam usage on Linux climbed past 5% in March, signaling growing gamer adoption on Linux platforms. Driven by Proton/Steam Deck and better driver support, this uptick could influence game porting decisions, driver development, and Linux ecosystem viability for gaming.</span><span class='zh'>三月 Linux 上 Steam 用户占比超过 5%，显示 Linux 平台的玩家采纳在增加。受 Proton/Steam Deck 和驱动支持改善推动，这一 uptick 可能影响游戏移植决策、驱动开发以及 Linux 的游戏生态可行性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ban the sale of precise geolocation</span><span class='zh'>是时候禁止精确地理位置数据的买卖</span></a><span class="dom">lawfaremedia.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer. It calls for tighter data-broker accountability, with attention to enforcement and alternatives for legitimate use cases like emergency services.</span><span class='zh'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。讨论执法难点与对紧急服务等正当用途的平衡，强调需要更严格的监管框架。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</span><span class='zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</span><span class='zh'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#d946ef' data-tag='Culture'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=hex.ooo&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>hex.ooo</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">105</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Isaac Asimov: The Last Question (1956)</span><span class='zh'>艾萨克·阿西莫夫：《最后的问题》（1956）</span></a><span class="dom">hex.ooo</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs. The narrative foreshadows core AI issues—scaling capabilities, interpretability, and the cost of pursuing unbounded optimization. It remains a touchstone for long-term thinking about technology&#x27;s reach and risks.</span><span class='zh'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。故事预示了 AI 扩展能力、可解释性以及追求无界优化的代价等核心议题。至今，它仍是关于技术影响及长期风险思考的重要参照。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Culture' style='--c:#d946ef'>Culture</span><span class='tag' data-t='Entertainment' style='--c:#c084fc'>Entertainment</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>732</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>288</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47792718" data-id="47792718" data-tags="Politics|Programming" data-score="720" data-comments="753" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718" data-time="1776346333">
<a class="thumb" href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f97316' data-tag='Politics'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=aphyr.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>aphyr.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">106</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Where do we go from here?</span><span class='zh'>未来一切都是谎言？我们该往何处走？</span></a><span class="dom">aphyr.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology.</span><span class='zh'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.claudecodecamp.com/p/i-measured-claude-4-7-s-new-tokenizer-here-s-what-it-costs-you" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Measuring Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs</span><span class='zh'>测量 Claude 4.7 的分词成本</span></a><span class="dom">claudecodecamp.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications. The analysis shows how tokenization shapes pricing, throughput, and model efficiency in real deployments. Takeaway: tokenization matters as much as model performance when budgeting AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。结论显示，在 AI 工作负载预算中，分词成本与模型性能同等重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thousands of rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive, expanding its treasure trove of live performances.</span><span class='zh'>数千份罕见的演唱会录音正加入互联网档案馆，丰富了其现场演出藏品。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ismy.blue/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is my blue your blue? (2024)</span><span class='zh'>我的蓝色是你的蓝色吗？</span></a><span class="dom">ismy.blue</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Color perception can vary across displays and lighting; this project experiments with blue to reveal subjective differences in hue naming and perception.</span><span class='zh'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Color perception can vary across displays and lighting; this project experiments with blue to reveal subjective differences in hue naming and perception. The result has implications for UI color choices, accessibility, and cross-device consistency in design.</span><span class='zh'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。对界面颜色选择、无障碍设计和跨设备的一致性具有启示意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>GoDaddy gave a domain to a stranger without any documentation</span><span class='zh'>GoDaddy 未经文档将域名转让给陌生人</span></a><span class="dom">anchor.host</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars. The incident underscores the risk of ownership confusion and the need for stronger authentication, audit trails, and post-transfer safeguards.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。此事凸显注册商在身份核验与转让审计方面的风险点，并强调需要更强的认证和纠纷处理机制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.mcdonalds.co.jp/en/menu/burger/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The buns in McDonald&#x27;s Japan&#x27;s burger photos are all slightly askew</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞漢堡照片中的麵包略微歪斜</span></a><span class="dom">mcdonalds.co.jp</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew. The misalignment may be a photography quirk or a production reality, but it sparks discussion about brand presentation versus the actual product. In an era of highly scrutinized visuals, tiny imperfections can become talking points about authenticity in marketing.</span><span class='zh'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。這種偏差可能是拍攝風格或製作過程造成，但卻引發對品牌呈現與實物之間差異的討論。在社群媒體高度放大的情境中，這種細微瑕疵也會成為關於真實感的話題。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.cloudflare.com/emdash-wordpress/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>EmDash – A spiritual successor to WordPress that solves plugin security</span><span class='zh'>EmDash：解决插件安全问题的 WordPress 精神续作</span></a><span class="dom">blog.cloudflare.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EmDash positions itself as a security-focused successor to WordPress, redesigning plugin architecture to reduce attack surface and improve isolation.</span><span class='zh'>EmDash 将自己定位为以安全为导向的 WordPress 继任者，通过重新设计插件架构与沙箱机制来降低攻击面并提升隔离性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EmDash positions itself as a security-focused successor to WordPress, redesigning plugin architecture to reduce attack surface and improve isolation. By reimagining plugin boundaries, it aims to deliver safer extensibility and better maintainability for developers and site owners.</span><span class='zh'>EmDash 将自己定位为以安全为导向的 WordPress 继任者，通过重新设计插件架构与沙箱机制来降低攻击面并提升隔离性。其目标是在保留插件扩展能力的同时，提高站点的安全性与可维护性，适合注重安全的开发者与站点管理员。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/4/28/before-github/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Before GitHub</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 之前</span></a><span class="dom">lucumr.pocoo.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools. The piece contrasts these workflows with modern platforms, emphasizing core needs like code review, issue tracking, and community governance.</span><span class='zh'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。与现代平台相比，文章强调代码审查、问题跟踪和社区治理等核心需求。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>660</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>223</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47943499" data-id="47943499" data-tags="Programming" data-score="658" data-comments="359" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499" data-time="1777429151">
<a class="thumb" href="https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='corrode.dev' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">122</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch</span><span class='zh'>Rust 捕捉不到的漏洞</span></a><span class="dom">corrode.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks. The post catalogs typical bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch and explains how to catch them with dynamic testing, fuzzing, and property-based checks. The author emphasizes that safety requires multi-layer defenses, not reliance on the compiler alone.</span><span class='zh'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。文章列出 Rust 捕捉不到的常见错误类型，并给出通过动态测试、模糊测试和属性检验等方法来弥补的做法。强调安全需要多层防护，而不仅依赖编译器。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>658</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>359</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47739313" data-id="47739313" data-tags="Energy" data-score="657" data-comments="473" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313" data-time="1776000099">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='the-independent.com' data-c='#eab308' data-tag='Energy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">123</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Seven countries now generate nearly all their electricity from renewables (2024)</span><span class='zh'>七个国家2024年几乎用可再生能源发电全部电力</span></a><span class="dom">the-independent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks. While intermittency remains a challenge, these cases show that with grid investments and capacity planning, a transition to low-carbon electricity is increasingly feasible at national scales.</span><span class='zh'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。尽管间歇性仍是挑战，但这些案例表明通过电网投资和容量规划，国家层面的低碳发电转型正变得更加可行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jack.cab/blog/every-firefox-extension" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Installing every* Firefox extension</span><span class='zh'>安装全部 Firefox 扩展</span></a><span class="dom">jack.cab</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author tests the Firefox extension ecosystem by installing every extension, documenting conflicts, performance, and sanity checks.</span><span class='zh'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author tests the Firefox extension ecosystem by installing every extension, documenting conflicts, performance, and sanity checks. The result reveals that the browser&#x27;s extension ecosystem is powerful but can become unwieldy, underscoring the need for better extension vetting and performance budgeting.</span><span class='zh'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。结果显示扩展生态强大却易变得难以维护，强调需要更严格的扩展筛选与性能预算。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/30/zig-anti-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Zig project&#x27;s rationale for their anti-AI contribution policy</span><span class='zh'>Zig 对 AI 贡献的明确反对立场及理由</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns. The policy emphasizes human-authored provenance and review as essential to maintaining code integrity.</span><span class='zh'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。该政策强调保持代码的人工源头和人工审查，以确保代码的完整性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://rivian.com/support/article/can-i-disable-all-data-collection-from-my-vehicle" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can I disable all data collection from my vehicle?</span><span class='zh'>我能否完全禁用车辆的数据收集？</span></a><span class="dom">rivian.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates.</span><span class='zh'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates. The Rivian guidance illustrates what can be turned off and what remains required for core functions like safety and navigation.</span><span class='zh'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。Rivian 的指南显示哪些数据可以关闭、哪些仍然被收集用于安全与导航等核心功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</span><span class='zh'>回归地道设计</span></a><span class="dom">essays.johnloeber.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen vs. ibuprofen</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚 vs. 布洛芬</span></a><span class="dom">asteriskmag.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles.</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles. Acetaminophen is gentler on the stomach but can damage the liver at high doses, while ibuprofen reduces inflammation but may upset the stomach and affect kidney function; dosing and medical context matter for safe use.</span><span class='zh'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。乙酰氨基酚对胃部影响较小，但高剂量可能损伤肝脏；布洛芬具有抗炎作用，但可能引起胃部不适并影响肾功能，使用时需考虑炎症程度、年龄和肝肾健康。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Health' style='--c:#4ade80'>Health</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>646</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>427</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47965060" data-id="47965060" data-tags="Privacy|Politics" data-score="639" data-comments="215" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060" data-time="1777567260">
<a class="thumb" href="https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=thereader.mitpress.mit.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>thereader.mitpress.mit.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">130</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How Mark Klein told the EFF about Room 641A [book excerpt]</span><span class='zh'>马克·克莱因向 EFF 披露641A室内幕的书摘</span></a><span class="dom">thereader.mitpress.mit.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance.</span><span class='zh'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance. The excerpt presents his firsthand account and the ensuing controversy, showing how insider disclosures can catalyze public scrutiny and policy debate around surveillance. It underscores the lasting impact whistleblowers have on privacy advocacy and reform efforts.</span><span class='zh'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。书摘给出他的第一手叙述及随后的争议，展示了内部披露如何点燃公众关注并推动 surveillance 相关的政策辩论。此事凸显举报人在隐私倡议与改革中的长期影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 pushes forward with improvements to ARM Mac compatibility, earlier boot issues, and new developer tooling. The project continues narrowing the gap between Apple Silicon hardware and open-source Linux.</span><span class='zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">132</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mozilla&#x27;s opposition to Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 反对 Chrome 的 Prompt API</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards. The stance highlights ongoing tensions between platform features, privacy safeguards, and interoperability in browser ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。这一立场凸显了浏览器生态中对隐私保护、功能设计与互操作性的持续博弈。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The local LLM ecosystem doesn’t need Ollama</span><span class='zh'>本地大模型生态不需要 Ollama</span></a><span class="dom">sleepingrobots.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance. It weighs trade-offs in updates, platform compatibility, and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。文章比较了更新、平台兼容性和生态成熟度等权衡，指出 Ollama 可能并非必需。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arhan.sh/blog/native-instant-space-switching-on-macos/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Native Instant Space Switching on macOS</span><span class='zh'>macOS 原生即时空间切换</span></a><span class="dom">arhan.sh</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces. It highlights implications for workflow efficiency, window management, and potential limitations compared with third-party tools.</span><span class='zh'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。文中讨论了这一特性对窗口管理和工作流程的影响，以及与第三方工具相比的潜在局限。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Meta&#x27;s move to remove ads linked to a social-media addiction lawsuit underscores how litigation is nudging platforms to rethink ad-supported models and user-well-being commitments. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://theonion.com/at-long-last-infowars-is-ours/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>At long last, InfoWars is ours</span><span class='zh'>InfoWars 终于落入我们手中</span></a><span class="dom">theonion.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems.</span><span class='zh'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems. It skewers how ownership can shape narratives and the culture around conspiracy sites, offering a meta take on media influence.</span><span class='zh'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。通过字句讽刺所有权如何塑造叙事，以及公众对阴谋论媒体的看法，折射出信息环境中的权力与影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ollama now supports MLX on Apple Silicon in preview, enabling accelerated on-device LLMs on M-series Macs.</span><span class='zh'>Ollama 现已在 Apple Silicon 支持 MLX，提供本地推理的加速效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ollama now supports MLX on Apple Silicon in preview, enabling accelerated on-device LLMs on M-series Macs. The preview could bring notable speedups and privacy benefits versus cloud inference, but adopters should be aware of early-stage status and hardware compatibility.</span><span class='zh'>Ollama 现已在 Apple Silicon 支持 MLX，提供本地推理的加速效果。预览版可能带来显著的性能提升和隐私优势，但仍处于早期阶段，需关注兼容性与稳定性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01193" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Embarrassingly simple self-distillation improves code generation</span><span class='zh'>苹果：极其简单的自蒸馏提升代码生成</span></a><span class="dom">arxiv.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A simple self-distillation technique improves code generation quality in experiments, suggesting a cheap method to boost model performance without large compute overhead.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示一种极其简单的自蒸馏方法就能提升代码生成质量，成本低且易于在现有模型上实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A simple self-distillation technique improves code generation quality in experiments, suggesting a cheap method to boost model performance without large compute overhead. It could be applied to training or inference of code assistants.</span><span class='zh'>研究显示一种极其简单的自蒸馏方法就能提升代码生成质量，成本低且易于在现有模型上实现。该方法对提升代码生成能力有潜在意义，可能为改进代码助手和编程相关任务提供可行路径。</span></p>
