<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cacm.acm.org/news/how-nasa-built-artemis-iis-fault-tolerant-computer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How NASA built Artemis II’s fault-tolerant computer</span><span class='zh'>NASA 如何打造 Artemis II 的容错计算机</span></a><span class="dom">cacm.acm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space. The system relies on redundancy, error detection, and hardened components to keep crewed flight safe even if primary hardware fails. The design illustrates how space-grade computing priorities differ from consumer systems.</span><span class='zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。该系统体现了太空计算对可靠性的高要求，远超商用系统的容错能力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Space' style='--c:#818cf8'>Space</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>616</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>222</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47606840" data-id="47606840" data-tags="Hardware" data-score="614" data-comments="529" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47606840" data-time="1775079401">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/raspberry-pi-price-increase-since-launch.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='jeffgeerling.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DRAM pricing is killing the hobbyist SBC market</span><span class='zh'>DRAM定价正在挤压爱好者级SBC市场</span></a><span class="dom">jeffgeerling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rising DRAM prices are squeezing the hobbyist SBC market, where memory is a core cost.</span><span class='zh'>DRAM价格上涨挤压了爱好者级SBC市场，内存成本成为关键负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rising DRAM prices are squeezing the hobbyist SBC market, where memory is a core cost. As memory becomes a larger share of BOM, many affordable boards struggle on margins and demand. The shift could push makers toward leaner designs or pre-bundled solutions.</span><span class='zh'>DRAM价格上涨挤压了爱好者级SBC市场，内存成本成为关键负担。随着内存占比上升，许多平价开发板的利润率和需求都被压缩。市场动荡可能促使厂商推出更节省内存的设计或打包解决方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-15/live-nation-illegally-monopolized-ticketing-market-jury-finds" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Live Nation illegally monopolized ticketing market, jury finds</span><span class='zh'>陪审团认定 Live Nation 垄断票务市场</span></a><span class="dom">bloomberg.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France plans to replace Windows with Linux on government devices to reduce reliance on US tech and tighten sovereignty. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/the-cult-of-vibe-coding-is-insane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The cult of vibe coding is dogfooding run amok</span><span class='zh'>氛围编码的崇拜：自我喂养文化失控</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality.</span><span class='zh'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality. It warns such culture can erode product reliability, real user outcomes, and long-run sustainability. The takeaway is to balance vibe with substance and user needs.</span><span class='zh'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。内部测试和满足感被当成质量指标，可能侵蚀产品可靠性、真实用户价值与长期可持续性。核心是要在氛围和实质、用户需求之间取得平衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains.</span><span class='zh'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains. It also highlights how precious metals markets react to sovereign reserve shifts.</span><span class='zh'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。这显示央行在分散储备时继续降低对单一托管方的依赖，并反映了资产保管的地缘政治考量。除此之外，此类举动往往会影响贵金属市场的供需和价格走向。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Argues that ML systems will exhibit unusual, sometimes counterintuitive behavior as data and objectives shift, complicating testing and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>指出机器学习系统在数据分布和目标变化下会呈现异常甚至反直觉的行为，这会让测试与可靠性变得复杂。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Argues that ML systems will exhibit unusual, sometimes counterintuitive behavior as data and objectives shift, complicating testing and reliability. The piece reflects on implications for software engineering, governance, and trust in AI.</span><span class='zh'>指出机器学习系统在数据分布和目标变化下会呈现异常甚至反直觉的行为，这会让测试与可靠性变得复杂。讨论对软件工程、治理与对 AI 信任的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://9to5mac.com/2026/04/09/fbi-used-iphone-notification-data-to-retrieve-deleted-signal-messages/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FBI used iPhone notification data to retrieve deleted Signal messages</span><span class='zh'>FBI 使用 iPhone 通知数据检索已删除的 Signal 消息</span></a><span class="dom">9to5mac.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions.</span><span class='zh'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions. This underscores a growing privacy and security risk for messaging apps, and could intensify debates over data minimization and platform protections.</span><span class='zh'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。此事凸显隐私与安全之间的博弈，或将推动关于数据最小化与平台保护的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.tangled.org/federation/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We need a federation of forges</span><span class='zh'>Tangled：我们需要一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络</span></a><span class="dom">blog.tangled.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration.</span><span class='zh'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration. It sketches a model for interoperable identities, permissions, and data exchange across platforms, with governance considerations for a healthy ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。文中勾画了在各个平台间实现互操作身份、权限与数据交换的模型，并就健康生态的治理提出思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonomi.dev/blog/color-code-your-bytes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Your hex editor should color-code bytes</span><span class='zh'>你的十六进制编辑器应对字节进行颜色编码</span></a><span class="dom">simonomi.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data. It discusses schemes for distinguishing text vs binary regions, endianness, and pattern detection.</span><span class='zh'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。它提出将 ASCII 区域、二进制数据、常见魔数等用不同颜色标示，以及如何辅助识别模式和字节序等细节。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion.</span><span class='zh'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion. They lower the barrier for homelabs, media servers, and USB-C powered devices to add fast Ethernet without PCIe NICs. Consider driver support and power draw when choosing a model.</span><span class='zh'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。无需 PCIe NIC 就能实现千兆级别的以太网速率，适合搭配 USB-C 设备使用。选购时留意驱动支持和供电需求。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023.</span><span class='zh'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023. The piece demonstrates how ultra-minimal digital art can echo a masterwork&#x27;s motion and drama, pushing the boundaries of what &#x27;low fidelity&#x27; can convey. It underscores the enduring appeal of classic images in the era of retro aesthetics and generative art.</span><span class='zh'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。極簡像素的限制放大波濤的動勢與張力，顯示經典名作在現代數位美學中的再創造力與吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://playstarfling.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Starfling: A one-tap endless orbital slingshot game in a single HTML file</span><span class='zh'>Starfling：一个 HTML 单文件的无尽轨道弹射游戏</span></a><span class="dom">playstarfling.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots.</span><span class='zh'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots. It showcases how far a tiny, self-contained web app can push interactivity and visuals with pure HTML/JS, without a build or asset pipeline. A fun example of ultra-lightweight game development and portability.</span><span class='zh'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。它演示了使用纯 HTML/JS 就能实现丰富交互与流畅画面的极致轻量化游戏，并且无需构建工具即可带来完整体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://letsdatascience.com/news/atlassian-enables-default-data-collection-to-train-ai-f71343d8" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Atlassian enables default data collection to train AI</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 启用默认数据收集以训练 AI</span></a><span class="dom">letsdatascience.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions.</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions. The move boosts AI-powered features but raises privacy, data governance, and policy questions for enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。这提升了 AI 驱动的功能，但也引发隐私、数据治理与企业政策方面的担忧。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mercor suffered a breach exposing 4TB of voice samples from about 40,000 AI contractors. The incident underscores the privacy and security risks in outsourced data-labeling workflows and could fuel debates about consent, governance, and stronger data handling controls.</span><span class='zh'>Mercor 的安全事件暴露了来自约4万名 AI 合作者的4 TB 语音样本。此事凸显外包数据标注流程中的隐私与安全风险，或促使对数据授权、治理与更强数据处理控制的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation. It argues for more robust, trustworthy benchmarks to guide research and product decisions.</span><span class='zh'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。它呼吁建立更可信的基准，以正确引导研究方向和产品决策。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</span><span class='zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</span></a><span class="dom">microsoft.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations.</span><span class='zh'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</span></a><span class="dom">archive.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://scottlawsonbc.com/post/dot-system" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A dot a day keeps the clutter away</span><span class='zh'>每天一个点，清除杂乱</span></a><span class="dom">scottlawsonbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A dot-a-day system is proposed to combat digital clutter by focusing on small, repeatable actions.</span><span class='zh'>提出每天一个点的管理体系以对抗数字杂乱，强调通过小而持续的行动来提升组织与专注度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A dot-a-day system is proposed to combat digital clutter by focusing on small, repeatable actions. The approach emphasizes low-friction habits that cumulatively improve organization.</span><span class='zh'>提出每天一个点的管理体系以对抗数字杂乱，强调通过小而持续的行动来提升组织与专注度。低摩擦的习惯可以逐步带来长期效果。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed.</span><span class='zh'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed. The thread highlights gaps in iOS security storytelling and urges stronger app review and user protections.</span><span class='zh'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。讨论聚焦 iOS 安全边界的薄弱点，呼吁加强应用审核和用户保护。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active.</span><span class='zh'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active. The appeal underscores ongoing challenges for volunteer-driven projects in sustaining development and support, even as mainstream clients rise. Donating helps ensure continued updates, privacy features, and localization support for users who value control over their data.</span><span class='zh'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。社区呼吁捐助以解决长期资金与维护挑战，确保未来仍具备更新、隐私功能及本地化支持。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/if-americas-so-rich-howd-it-get-so" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If America&#x27;s so rich, how&#x27;d it get so sad?</span><span class='zh'>美国这么富有，怎会变得如此悲伤？</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</span><span class='zh'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。文章探讨经济增长、社会福祉之间的错配，以及技术（包括 AI）对这种关系的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software.</span><span class='zh'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software. The initiative aims to accelerate modernization, improve security through collaborative development, and reduce vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。此举旨在加速公共部门软件现代化、提升安全性并减少厂商锁定。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/04/30/10" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>For Linux kernel vulnerabilities, there is no heads-up to distributions</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞：对发行版没有事先通知</span></a><span class="dom">openwall.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. The situation highlights gaps in how advisories propagate from researchers to downstream users, affecting enterprise risk management.</span><span class='zh'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。这暴露了研究人员、公告渠道与发行版维护之间的信息传递断层，影响企业的风险管理和修补节奏。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lists.zx2c4.com/pipermail/wireguard/2026-April/009561.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WireGuard makes new Windows release following Microsoft signing resolution</span><span class='zh'>WireGuard 在微软签名决定后推出全新 Windows 版本</span></a><span class="dom">lists.zx2c4.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles.</span><span class='zh'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles. The update improves installer reliability and kernel-mode VPN performance, underscoring how platform signing policies shape open-source tooling on Windows.</span><span class='zh'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。更新提升安装可靠性与内核模式下的性能，体现了平台签名策略对开源工具在 Windows 上的影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available.</span><span class='zh'>一位密码学工程师对量子计算的时间线给出务实的看法，认为要实现实用的量子攻击仍需远多于现有的量子比特与纠错能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AMD&#x27;s Lemonade project showcases a fast, open-source local LLM server that uses both GPUs and NPUs to run offline, private inference.</span><span class='zh'>AMD 的 Lemonade 项目是一个快速、开源的本地大语言模型服务器，利用 GPU 和 NPU 实现离线私有推理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wesnoth.org" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Battle for Wesnoth: open-source, turn-based strategy game</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth：开源的回合制策略游戏</span></a><span class="dom">wesnoth.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns.</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns. The project demonstrates how community-driven development can sustain a complex strategy title, with robust modding support and cross-platform availability. Its enduring viability highlights the strength of open-source ecosystems for niche, mechanics-heavy games.</span><span class='zh'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。项目展示了开源社区如何支撑复杂策略游戏的持续开发，以及跨平台和可扩展的模组生态。它的长期活力凸显了开源生态在维护高复杂性游戏中的作用。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='dbreunig.com' data-c='#ef4444' data-tag='Security'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">185</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity looks like proof of work now</span><span class='zh'>網路安全如今看起來像工作量證明</span></a><span class="dom">dbreunig.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics. Defenders must continually invest to detect, deter, and recover from breaches, creating an ongoing arms race with threat actors. This perspective helps explain why security budgets tend to expand with rising threat capabilities.</span><span class='zh'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。防禦方需要不斷投入資源來偵測、阻擊與修復破口，與威脅方的能力共同推動成本上升。以此觀點看待企業安全預算的成長具有說服力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>529</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>197</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47736476" data-id="47736476" data-tags="AI|Code" data-score="524" data-comments="400" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476" data-time="1775972752">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">186</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</span><span class='zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.inaturalist.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>iNaturalist</span><span class='zh'>iNaturalist 自然观测平台</span></a><span class="dom">inaturalist.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>iNaturalist enables crowdsourced biodiversity data from millions of users, driving research and conservation.</span><span class='zh'>iNaturalist 通过海量用户的群体观测，推动生物多样性研究与保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>iNaturalist enables crowdsourced biodiversity data from millions of users, driving research and conservation. Its open data catalog helps scientists track species distributions and ecological changes over time. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://worksinprogress.co/issue/why-japan-has-such-good-railways/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why Japan has such good railways</span><span class='zh'>日本铁路为何如此出色</span></a><span class="dom">worksinprogress.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores factors behind Japan&#x27;s top-tier rail network: meticulous maintenance, on-time performance, dense urban planning, and integrated operations.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析日本铁路的四大支撑：精细维护、准点运营、密集的城市规划以及一体化运营。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores factors behind Japan&#x27;s top-tier rail network: meticulous maintenance, on-time performance, dense urban planning, and integrated operations. The piece shows how culture, funding, and technology converge to deliver reliable service.</span><span class='zh'>本文分析日本铁路的四大支撑：精细维护、准点运营、密集的城市规划以及一体化运营。作者指出文化、资金投入与技术协同共同铸就了高水平的铁路服务。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Polymarket bot consistently places &#x27;No&#x27; bets on non-sports markets, revealing a recurring pattern in market dynamics that automated strategies can exploit. This raises questions about prediction-market design and how such bots can skew odds or drain liquidity.</span><span class='zh'>一个 Polymarket 机器人在非体育市场持续以‘否’下注，暴露了市场机制中的漏洞和自动化策略的潜在影响。此现象引发对预测市场设计、赔率偏差以及流动性消耗的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/matthartman/ghost-pepper" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Ghost Pepper – Local hold-to-talk speech-to-text for macOS</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Ghost Pepper——本地按住说话的语音转文本（macOS）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghost Pepper offers on-device speech-to-text for macOS, activated by a hold-to-talk gesture.</span><span class='zh'>Ghost Pepper 在 macOS 上实现本地语音转文本，按住说话即可转写。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghost Pepper offers on-device speech-to-text for macOS, activated by a hold-to-talk gesture. By running locally, it preserves privacy and avoids cloud latency, appealing to developers building offline workflows or privacy-focused apps. This project could inspire tighter macOS integration for quick transcription without internet calls.</span><span class='zh'>Ghost Pepper 在 macOS 上实现本地语音转文本，按住说话即可转写。无需云端上传，强调隐私和离线使用，适合需要快速、低延迟转写的工作流。对想在系统层面集成转写功能的开发者来说，具有潜在的轻量化方向。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Keychron/Keychron-Keyboards-Hardware-Design" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Industrial design files for Keychron keyboards and mice</span><span class='zh'>Keychron 键盘和鼠标的工业设计档案公开</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Keychron has open-sourced industrial design files for its keyboards and mice, inviting DIY fabrication, replication, and community-driven hardware tinkering.</span><span class='zh'>Keychron 公开了键盘和鼠标的工业设计档案，降低了自制、改装和硬件透明度的门槛。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Keychron has open-sourced industrial design files for its keyboards and mice, inviting DIY fabrication, replication, and community-driven hardware tinkering. The move lowers barrier to modification, audits, and transparency, while highlighting how hardware design data can accelerate innovation.</span><span class='zh'>Keychron 公开了键盘和鼠标的工业设计档案，降低了自制、改装和硬件透明度的门槛。此举推动开源硬件社区的发展，同时也让设计数据更易于审计与创新。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pgbackrest/pgbackrest" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pgbackrest is no longer being maintained</span><span class='zh'>Pgbackrest 已不再维护</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pgbackrest maintainers announced it&#x27;s no longer actively maintained, urging users to consider alternatives or forks.</span><span class='zh'>Pgbackrest 的维护者宣布不再进行积极维护，建议用户考虑替代方案或分支。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pgbackrest maintainers announced it&#x27;s no longer actively maintained, urging users to consider alternatives or forks. This raises questions about data integrity, patching, and support for PostgreSQL backup tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Pgbackrest 的维护者宣布不再进行积极维护，建议用户考虑替代方案或分支。这使 PostgreSQL 备份工具的安全性、补丁和支持问题变得突出。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yuvadm/quantumslop/blob/25ad2e76ae58baa96f6219742459407db9dd17f5/URANDOM_DEMO.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Replace IBM Quantum back end with /dev/urandom</span><span class='zh'>用 /dev/urandom 替换 IBM 量子后端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A playful demo replaces the IBM Quantum backend with /dev/urandom, showing that for some workflows, classical randomness can mimic quantum results well enough to test software.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的演示用 /dev/urandom 来替代 IBM 量子后端，表明在某些工作流中，经典随机性也能在测试阶段近似量子结果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A playful demo replaces the IBM Quantum backend with /dev/urandom, showing that for some workflows, classical randomness can mimic quantum results well enough to test software. The exercise highlights the importance of trustworthy backends and clear expectations around quantum versus classical randomness.</span><span class='zh'>一个有趣的演示用 /dev/urandom 来替代 IBM 量子后端，表明在某些工作流中，经典随机性也能在测试阶段近似量子结果。此举强调信任的后端与区分量子与经典随机性的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/matz/spinel" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spinel: Ruby AOT Native Compiler</span><span class='zh'>Spinel：Ruby 的 AOT 原生编译器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Spinel is an ahead-of-time native compiler for Ruby, precompiling Ruby code into optimized machine code.</span><span class='zh'>Spinel 是 Ruby 的 AOT 原生编译器，将 Ruby 代码预编译为本地机器码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Spinel is an ahead-of-time native compiler for Ruby, precompiling Ruby code into optimized machine code. The compiler promises faster startup times and lower memory usage, benefiting server apps, scripting tools, and embedded deployments. If it matures, Spinel could shift Ruby performance characteristics closer to statically compiled languages.</span><span class='zh'>Spinel 是 Ruby 的 AOT 原生编译器，将 Ruby 代码预编译为本地机器码。它有望提高启动速度并降低内存占用，受益于服务器应用、脚本工具和嵌入式部署。若发展成熟，Spinel 可能使 Ruby 的性能更接近静态编译语言。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/i12bp8/TagTinker" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Edit store price tags using Flipper Zero</span><span class='zh'>用 Flipper Zero 修改商店价格标签</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Editing store price tags with Flipper Zero demonstrates potential tampering of NFC/RFID labels in retail.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Flipper Zero 编辑商店价格标签演示了零售环境下对近场通信/射频标签的潜在篡改风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Editing store price tags with Flipper Zero demonstrates potential tampering of NFC/RFID labels in retail. The project highlights security risks in automated tagging and stresses the need for tamper-evident controls. It serves as a reminder to harden retail IoT and labeling systems.</span><span class='zh'>使用 Flipper Zero 编辑商店价格标签演示了零售环境下对近场通信/射频标签的潜在篡改风险。该项目凸显了自动标签的安全隐患，并强调防篡改控制的重要性，提醒业界加强零售物联网和标签系统的安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/hauntsaninja/git_bayesect" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Git bayesect – Bayesian Git bisection for non-deterministic bugs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Git bayesect—用于非确定性缺陷的贝叶斯二分定位</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Git bayesect applies Bayesian reasoning to Git bisect, helping locate flaky/non-deterministic bugs when traditional bisect stalls.</span><span class='zh'>Git bayesect 使用贝叶斯推理来处理非确定性缺陷的回溯问题，当传统的二分回溯在测试不稳定时难以定位时尤为有用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Git bayesect applies Bayesian reasoning to Git bisect, helping locate flaky/non-deterministic bugs when traditional bisect stalls. It guides the search with probabilistic updates, potentially saving triage time.</span><span class='zh'>Git bayesect 使用贝叶斯推理来处理非确定性缺陷的回溯问题，当传统的二分回溯在测试不稳定时难以定位时尤为有用。通过对提交的概率更新引导搜索，可以缩短定位时间。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-research/timesfm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Google&#x27;s 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with 16k context</span><span class='zh'>谷歌发布 200M 参数的时序基础模型，具 16k 上下文容量</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Google Research unveils TimesFM, a 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with a 16k context window.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌研究院发布 TimesFM，一个200M参数的时序基础模型，具备16k上下文窗口。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Google Research unveils TimesFM, a 200M-parameter time-series foundation model with a 16k context window. The model demonstrates that compact architectures can achieve long-range forecasting, unlocking more capable edge and on-device time-series analytics with lower compute budgets.</span><span class='zh'>谷歌研究院发布 TimesFM，一个200M参数的时序基础模型，具备16k上下文窗口。该模型表明紧凑架构也能实现长距离预测，推动边缘和本地时序分析在更低算力预算下实现。</span></p>

For researchers and developers who want offline, portable knowledge management, it&#x27;s a compelling option.</span><span class='zh'>Tolaria 是一个开源的 macOS 应用，用来管理 Markdown 知识库。它支持本地存储、Markdown 编辑，以及笔记之间的连结，方便构建可搜索的知识体系。对需要离线、可移植笔记管理的开发者和研究者很有吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/russellromney/honker" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Honker – Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN Semantics for SQLite</span><span class='zh'>Honker：SQLite 实现 Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN 语义</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Honker brings Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN semantics to SQLite, enabling real-time event signaling without a full Postgres server.</span><span class='zh'>Honker 将 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 实时通知语义带到 SQLite，使嵌入式应用能够在不依赖完整 Postgres 的情况下实现事件驱动通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Honker brings Postgres NOTIFY/LISTEN semantics to SQLite, enabling real-time event signaling without a full Postgres server. It demonstrates how lightweight, embedded databases can emulate cross-database messaging patterns, useful for event-driven UIs and local apps.</span><span class='zh'>Honker 将 PostgreSQL 的 LISTEN/NOTIFY 实时通知语义带到 SQLite，使嵌入式应用能够在不依赖完整 Postgres 的情况下实现事件驱动通信。它为本地应用提供了轻量级的事件总线，适合实现实时 UI 更新等场景。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Karpathy&#x27;s LLM Wiki presents an &#x27;idea file&#x27; blueprint to organize prompts, snippets, and evolving strategies for working with large language models. It offers practical patterns for building a personal knowledge base to speed AI-centric development.</span><span class='zh'>Karpathy 的 LLM Wiki 将“idea file”方法用于整理提示、片段和应对大模型的策略，帮助建立可持续的知识库以提升 AI 驱动的开发效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL/pull/15377" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SDL Now Supports DOS</span><span class='zh'>SDL 现已支持 DOS 环境</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SDL now supports DOS environments, enabling legacy DOS apps to run with modern SDL features.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 现已支持 DOS 环境，使基于 SDL 的旧 DOS 应用能够在现代系统上运行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SDL now supports DOS environments, enabling legacy DOS apps to run with modern SDL features. This broadens cross-platform reach for retro games and tooling built on SDL, and could simplify porting older multimedia software.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 现已支持 DOS 环境，使基于 SDL 的旧 DOS 应用能够在现代系统上运行。此举拓展了向后兼容性，有助于复兴复古游戏和相关工具的移植与开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/callumlocke/json-formatter" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>JSON formatter Chrome plugin now closed and injecting adware</span><span class='zh'>Chrome JSON Formatter 插件现已关闭，且被注入广告软件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Chrome extension formerly known as JSON Formatter has been shut down after revelations that it injected adware into users&#x27; browsers.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome JSON Formatter 插件已被关闭，同时被证实注入广告软件，影响用户浏览体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Chrome extension formerly known as JSON Formatter has been shut down after revelations that it injected adware into users&#x27; browsers. This underscores ongoing security risks in browser extensions and the need for careful vetting of third-party tools.</span><span class='zh'>Chrome JSON Formatter 插件已被关闭，同时被证实注入广告软件，影响用户浏览体验。事件凸显浏览器扩展的安全风险，强调对第三方工具的审查与权限控制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fikrikarim/parlor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Real-time AI (audio/video in, voice out) on an M3 Pro with Gemma E2B</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 M3 Pro + Gemma E2B 上实现的实时 AI（音视频输入/语音输出）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces a real-time AI pipeline that handles audio/video input and outputs voice on an Apple M3 Pro using Gemma E2B accelerators.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在苹果 M3 Pro 上利用 Gemma E2B 加速器实现的实时 AI 流程，涵盖音视频输入和语音输出，强调边缘计算下的低延迟多媒体处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN introduces a real-time AI pipeline that handles audio/video input and outputs voice on an Apple M3 Pro using Gemma E2B accelerators. It demonstrates efficient edge AI with low latency suitable for portable workloads. This serves as a practical blueprint for on-device AI without cloud round-trips.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了在苹果 M3 Pro 上利用 Gemma E2B 加速器实现的实时 AI 流程，涵盖音视频输入和语音输出，强调边缘计算下的低延迟多媒体处理。该实现为无需云端往返的设备端 AI 提供了务实范本。</span></p>

It serves as a playful showcase of higher-dimensional computation and visualization.</span><span class='zh'>4D Doom 项目将 Doom 推向四维空间，探索在高维空间中的渲染与导航。是对高维计算和可视化的一种有趣概念实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/axios/axios/issues/10636" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Post Mortem: axios NPM supply chain compromise</span><span class='zh'>Axios 的 NPM 供应链安全事件事后分析</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Axios experienced a supply-chain compromise on npm, with a malicious package version slipping into the ecosystem and executing unsafe code on install.</span><span class='zh'>Axios 遭遇 npm 供应链入侵，某个恶意版本在发布后被广泛使用并执行恶意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Axios experienced a supply-chain compromise on npm, with a malicious package version slipping into the ecosystem and executing unsafe code on install. The post-mortem outlines how the breach happened, the remediation steps taken, and key lessons for preventing future incidents.</span><span class='zh'>Axios 遭遇 npm 供应链入侵，某个恶意版本在发布后被广泛使用并执行恶意代码。分析稿梳理了漏洞的成因、应对措施以及未来防护要点。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Security researcher Claude demonstrates a full remote kernel RCE on FreeBSD with a root shell, highlighting a serious vulnerability chain in the kernel.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究员 Claude 展示了对 FreeBSD 的一次完整远程内核远程代码执行，获得 root shell，暴露内核层级的严重漏洞链。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Security researcher Claude demonstrates a full remote kernel RCE on FreeBSD with a root shell, highlighting a serious vulnerability chain in the kernel. The disclosure underscores the need for timely patching, network segmentation, and hardened kernel configurations to reduce risk.</span><span class='zh'>安全研究员 Claude 展示了对 FreeBSD 的一次完整远程内核远程代码执行，获得 root shell，暴露内核层级的严重漏洞链。该披露强调及时打补丁、网络分段和强化内核配置以降低风险的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/calcom/cal.diy" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cal.diy: open-source community edition of cal.com</span><span class='zh'>Cal.diy：cal.com 的开源社区版</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cal.diy is a community edition of cal.com that you can self-host, enabling scheduling on your own infrastructure with privacy controls.</span><span class='zh'>Cal.diy 是 cal.com 的开源社区版，支持自托管，让团队在自有基础设施上进行排程并掌控隐私。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cal.diy is a community edition of cal.com that you can self-host, enabling scheduling on your own infrastructure with privacy controls. It demonstrates how open-source forks empower teams to customize and avoid vendor lock-in. The project broadens choices for privacy-conscious deployments.</span><span class='zh'>Cal.diy 是 cal.com 的开源社区版，支持自托管，让团队在自有基础设施上进行排程并掌控隐私。它展示了开源分叉如何赋能定制化并减少对厂商的依赖，为注重隐私的部署提供了更多选择。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/fabienmillet/WiiFin" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WiiFin – Jellyfin Client for Nintendo Wii</span><span class='zh'>WiiFin：面向 Nintendo Wii 的 Jellyfin 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WiiFin is a Jellyfin client for the Nintendo Wii, enabling media streaming on retro hardware.</span><span class='zh'>WiiFin 是面向 Nintendo Wii 的 Jellyfin 客户端，允许在这台老旧主机上从 Jellyfin 服务器流媒体。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WiiFin is a Jellyfin client for the Nintendo Wii, enabling media streaming on retro hardware. It demonstrates how open-source media projects extend to older consoles, though the experience is constrained by the platform&#x27;s limits.</span><span class='zh'>WiiFin 是面向 Nintendo Wii 的 Jellyfin 客户端，允许在这台老旧主机上从 Jellyfin 服务器流媒体。该项目展示了开源媒体生态向旧硬件的扩展潜力，但受限于硬件性能与控制体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ArcaneNibble/awawausb" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WebUSB Extension for Firefox</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 的 WebUSB 扩展</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Firefox WebUSB extension enables web apps to access USB devices directly, expanding browser hardware access while raising security considerations.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 的 WebUSB 扩展使网页应用能够直接访问 USB 设备，在扩展浏览器对硬件的接入能力的同时，也带来安全方面的考量，体现了将硬件互操作性带到网页的持续努力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Firefox WebUSB extension enables web apps to access USB devices directly, expanding browser hardware access while raising security considerations. This work illustrates ongoing efforts to bring hardware interoperability to the web.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 的 WebUSB 扩展使网页应用能够直接访问 USB 设备，在扩展浏览器对硬件的接入能力的同时，也带来安全方面的考量，体现了将硬件互操作性带到网页的持续努力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gastownhall/gastown/issues/3649" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Does Gas Town &#x27;steal&#x27; usage from users&#x27; LLM credits to improve itself?</span><span class='zh'>Gas Town 是否从用户的 LLM 使用额度窃取来提升自身？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The discussion questions whether Gas Town misuses users&#x27; LLM usage credits to improve its own models, pointing to opaque incentives and data usage policies.</span><span class='zh'>讨论质疑 Gas Town 是否在未告知的情况下从用户的 LLM 使用额度中获利提升自身模型，指出激励与数据使用政策不透明。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The discussion questions whether Gas Town misuses users&#x27; LLM usage credits to improve its own models, pointing to opaque incentives and data usage policies. It argues for greater transparency, auditability, and clear user consent.</span><span class='zh'>讨论质疑 Gas Town 是否在未告知的情况下从用户的 LLM 使用额度中获利提升自身模型，指出激励与数据使用政策不透明。呼吁增加透明度、可审计性以及明确的用户同意。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Hanqaqa/Easyduino" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Easyduino: Open Source PCB Devboards for KiCad</span><span class='zh'>Easyduino：面向 KiCad 的开源 PCB 开发板</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Easyduino offers open-source PCB dev boards designed for KiCad, enabling hobbyists and educators to prototype Arduino-compatible hardware with turnkey PCB layouts.</span><span class='zh'>Easyduino 提供面向 KiCad 的开源 PCB 开发板，使爱好者和教育工作者能够用现成版图快速实现 Arduino 兼容硬件原型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Easyduino offers open-source PCB dev boards designed for KiCad, enabling hobbyists and educators to prototype Arduino-compatible hardware with turnkey PCB layouts. The project lowers barriers to hardware iteration and fosters an ecosystem around KiCad-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Easyduino 提供面向 KiCad 的开源 PCB 开发板，使爱好者和教育工作者能够用现成版图快速实现 Arduino 兼容硬件原型。该项目降低硬件迭代门槛，促进 KiCad 友好工作流的生态发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/49363" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Regression: malware reminder on every read still causes subagent refusals</span><span class='zh'>回归：每次读取时的恶意软件提醒仍导致子代理拒绝</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A regression in AI tooling causes malware-reminder prompts to fire on every read, triggering subagents to refuse tasks.</span><span class='zh'>AI 工具链中出现回归：每次读取都会触发恶意软件提醒，导致子代理拒绝执行任务。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A regression in AI tooling causes malware-reminder prompts to fire on every read, triggering subagents to refuse tasks. The behavior highlights the tension between aggressive safety prompts and workflow usability. It suggests tighter prompt control and more robust delegation logic to avoid blocking legitimate operations.</span><span class='zh'>AI 工具链中出现回归：每次读取都会触发恶意软件提醒，导致子代理拒绝执行任务。这体现出安全提醒的强硬风格与工作流可用性之间的张力。文章建议更精确的提示控制和更健壮的任务分发逻辑，以避免阻塞正常操作。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/04/28/top_stories_04282026'><span class='en'>Apr 28</span><span class='zh'>4月28日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>249</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47942492' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>144</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47807809" data-id="47807809" data-tags="Show HN|Privacy|Code" data-score="249" data-comments="111" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807809" data-time="1776443924">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/paniclock/paniclock/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: PanicLock – Close your MacBook lid disable TouchID –&gt; password unlock</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock：合上 MacBook 盖时禁用 Touch ID，改用密码解锁</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PanicLock is a small utility that disables Touch ID when the MacBook lid is closed and requires a password to unlock.</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock 是一个小型工具，合上 MacBook 盖时会禁用指纹解锁，解锁需输入密码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PanicLock is a small utility that disables Touch ID when the MacBook lid is closed and requires a password to unlock. It provides a simple privacy safeguard for scenarios like lending or transporting devices, though it adds an extra unlock step.</span><span class='zh'>PanicLock 是一个小型工具，合上 MacBook 盖时会禁用指纹解锁，解锁需输入密码。这为借出或携带设备等场景提供了简单的隐私保护，但也增加了额外的解锁步骤。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Niri 26.04 release introduces scrollable tiling in its Wayland compositor, enabling more flexible window tiling with scrollable frames.</span><span class='zh'>Niri 26.04 版本在 Wayland 合成器中加入可滚动的平铺布局，使窗口排布更加灵活，支持滚动的窗口区域。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Niri 26.04 release introduces scrollable tiling in its Wayland compositor, enabling more flexible window tiling with scrollable frames. The update improves multitasking for users who prefer tiling workflows and dynamic navigation.</span><span class='zh'>Niri 26.04 版本在 Wayland 合成器中加入可滚动的平铺布局，使窗口排布更加灵活，支持滚动的窗口区域。此更新提升了偏好平铺工作流的用户在多任务和导航上的体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rochus-keller/OberonSystem3Native/releases" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Oberon System 3 runs natively on Raspberry Pi 3 (with ready SD card)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Oberon System 3 可原生在 Raspberry Pi 3 上运行（含现成 SD 卡）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A demo shows Oberon System 3 running natively on Raspberry Pi 3, accompanied by a ready-to-use SD card image.</span><span class='zh'>演示展示 Oberon System 3 在 Raspberry Pi 3 上原生运行，并提供现成的 SD 卡镜像。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A demo shows Oberon System 3 running natively on Raspberry Pi 3, accompanied by a ready-to-use SD card image. It demonstrates how a compact, single-tasking OS can still be useful for education and experimentation on modern SBCs.</span><span class='zh'>演示展示 Oberon System 3 在 Raspberry Pi 3 上原生运行，并提供现成的 SD 卡镜像。此举显示了简约、单任务操作系统在现代单板计算机上的教育与试验潜力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN presenting Auto-Architecture, a loop-based system inspired by Karpathy that automatically designs and evaluates neural network architectures on commodity CPU hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示 Auto-Architecture，这是一套受 Karpathy 思路启发、在普通 CPU 硬件上自动设计与评估神经网络架构的循环系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN presenting Auto-Architecture, a loop-based system inspired by Karpathy that automatically designs and evaluates neural network architectures on commodity CPU hardware. It demonstrates end-to-end automation of architecture search without GPU dependence. The project highlights the growing appeal of accessible, automated ML design workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示 Auto-Architecture，这是一套受 Karpathy 思路启发、在普通 CPU 硬件上自动设计与评估神经网络架构的循环系统。它演示了无需 GPU 的端到端架构自动化搜索。该项目凸显了可负担性与自动化 ML 设计流程的日益吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ocaml/ocaml/pull/14701" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A new C++ back end for ocamlc</span><span class='zh'>Ocamlc 的 C++ 后端新实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A new C++ backend for the OCaml compiler backend (ocamlc) appears, enabling code generation or optimization paths using C++-based infrastructure.</span><span class='zh'>OCaml 编译器后端（ocamlc）新增了 C++ 后端，可能带来通过 C++ 基础设施进行代码生成与优化的路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A new C++ backend for the OCaml compiler backend (ocamlc) appears, enabling code generation or optimization paths using C++-based infrastructure. If realized, it could improve portability, performance, and interoperability between OCaml and C++ ecosystems, influencing language tooling and compiler design.</span><span class='zh'>OCaml 编译器后端（ocamlc）新增了 C++ 后端，可能带来通过 C++ 基础设施进行代码生成与优化的路径。若落地，将提升可移植性与性能，并加强 OCaml 与 C++ 生态的互操作性，对语言工具链与编译器设计产生影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gemma 4 is a multimodal model fine-tuner designed to run on Apple Silicon, enabling on-device training and fine-tuning without sending data to the cloud.</span><span class='zh'>Gemma 4 是为 Apple Silicon 设计的多模态模型微调工具，支持在设备端进行模型微调，无需将数据上传云端。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/44257" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code login fails with OAuth timeout on Windows</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 在 Windows 上因 OAuth 超时而无法登录</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An issue reports Claude Code login failing on Windows due to OAuth timeout, highlighting cross‑platform authentication challenges for AI copilots.</span><span class='zh'>一个问题报告指出在 Windows 上使用 Claude Code 时因 OAuth 超时而无法登录，暴露了跨平台认证的挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An issue reports Claude Code login failing on Windows due to OAuth timeout, highlighting cross‑platform authentication challenges for AI copilots. Workarounds exist, and the incident underscores the need for robust, user-friendly sign-in flows across OSes.</span><span class='zh'>一个问题报告指出在 Windows 上使用 Claude Code 时因 OAuth 超时而无法登录，暴露了跨平台认证的挑战。常见的解决方法存在，这次事件强调了在不同操作系统上提供稳健、易用的登录流程的必要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/raysan5/raylib/releases/tag/6.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Raylib v6.0</span><span class='zh'>Raylib 6.0 发布</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Raylib 6.0 arrives as a major update to the lightweight game development library, offering API improvements, better portability, and streamlined tooling for cross-platform projects.</span><span class='zh'>Raylib 6.0 作为轻量级游戏开发库的重大更新，带来 API 改善、可移植性提升与跨平台工具链的简化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Raylib 6.0 arrives as a major update to the lightweight game development library, offering API improvements, better portability, and streamlined tooling for cross-platform projects. The release makes it easier for indie developers and educators to build and teach with graphics and audio capabilities. It could accelerate rapid prototyping and learning in game development communities.</span><span class='zh'>Raylib 6.0 作为轻量级游戏开发库的重大更新，带来 API 改善、可移植性提升与跨平台工具链的简化。该版本让独立开发者与教育者更容易构建并教学，强化图形、音频等功能。预计将推动快速原型设计和游戏开发社区的学习进程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ENTERPILOT/GOModel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: GoModel – an open-source AI gateway in Go</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: GoModel——用 Go 实现的开源 AI 网关；比 LiteLLM 轻量 44 倍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GoModel is a lightweight open-source AI gateway written in Go, boasting a footprint far smaller than competing LLM gateways.</span><span class='zh'>GoModel 是用 Go 编写的轻量级开源 AI 网关，声称体积要比 LiteLLM 等实现低几十倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GoModel is a lightweight open-source AI gateway written in Go, boasting a footprint far smaller than competing LLM gateways. It targets easier deployment of small-to-midscale AI services, especially for edge or on-prem setups.</span><span class='zh'>GoModel 是用 Go 编写的轻量级开源 AI 网关，声称体积要比 LiteLLM 等实现低几十倍。它面向在边缘或本地部署的小到中等规模的 AI 服务，降低门槛与运维成本。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AndrewVos/endless-toil" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hear your agent suffer through your code</span><span class='zh'>让你的智能体在你的代码里痛苦地折腾</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An experimental setup where an AI agent repeatedly runs and debugs code, exposing how brittle software and ambiguous interfaces force costly problem-framing.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个让人工智能代理不断执行、调试代码的实验场景，揭示了脆弱代码和模糊接口会带来高成本的问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An experimental setup where an AI agent repeatedly runs and debugs code, exposing how brittle software and ambiguous interfaces force costly problem-framing. It underscores the need for reliable tooling, better error messages, and structured prompts to reduce toil when AI-assisted coding.</span><span class='zh'>这是一个让人工智能代理不断执行、调试代码的实验场景，揭示了脆弱代码和模糊接口会带来高成本的问题。文章强调需要更可靠的工具、清晰的错误信息，以及结构化的提示以降低 AI 辅助编码时的工作量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mikf/gallery-dl/discussions/9304" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Media scraper Gallery-dl is moving to Codeberg after receiving a DMCA notice</span><span class='zh'>Gallery-dl 媒体抓取工具在收到 DMCA 通知后迁往 Codeberg</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gallery-dl, a popular media-scraping tool, is moving its repository to Codeberg after a DMCA takedown notice.</span><span class='zh'>知名媒体抓取工具 Gallery-dl 在收到 DMCA 通知后将其代码库迁移至 Codeberg。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gallery-dl, a popular media-scraping tool, is moving its repository to Codeberg after a DMCA takedown notice. This underscores how hosting choices can pressure OSS projects to relocate, and highlights Codeberg as an appealing alternative for projects seeking EU-based hosting and privacy-forward policies.</span><span class='zh'>知名媒体抓取工具 Gallery-dl 在收到 DMCA 通知后将其代码库迁移至 Codeberg。这凸显开源项目在托管平台选择上的压力，以及 Codeberg 作为欧洲基地更注重隐私与合规性的潜在吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shivampkumar/trellis-mac" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Run TRELLIS.2 Image-to-3D generation natively on Apple Silicon</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：在 Apple Silicon 上原生运行 TRELLIS.2 的图像转3D 生成</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TRELLIS.2 enables image-to-3D generation, and this build runs natively on Apple Silicon, suggesting optimized performance for Macs with M-series chips.</span><span class='zh'>TRELLIS.2 实现图像到3D 的生成，且在 Apple Silicon 上实现原生运行，表现在对 M1/M2 的优化能显著提升速度与离线工作能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TRELLIS.2 enables image-to-3D generation, and this build runs natively on Apple Silicon, suggesting optimized performance for Macs with M-series chips. This lowers hardware barriers for offline, on-device AI content creation and could influence Mac-focused AI tooling.</span><span class='zh'>TRELLIS.2 实现图像到3D 的生成，且在 Apple Silicon 上实现原生运行，表现在对 M1/M2 的优化能显著提升速度与离线工作能力。此举降低了对高端云算力的依赖，利于本地创作和跨平台工具链的开发。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/magiblot/tvision" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Turbo Vision 2.0 – a modern port</span><span class='zh'>Turbo Vision 2.0：现代移植</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbo Vision 2.0 is a modern port that keeps the classic terminal UI library alive with updated builds and cross-platform support.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo Vision 2.0 是对经典终端 UI 库的现代移植，更新构建并扩展跨平台支持。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbo Vision 2.0 is a modern port that keeps the classic terminal UI library alive with updated builds and cross-platform support. It enables lightweight, text-based interfaces for tools and utilities, appealing to retro enthusiasts and systems programmers. The project demonstrates how legacy tooling can evolve without abandoning its roots.</span><span class='zh'>Turbo Vision 2.0 是对经典终端 UI 库的现代移植，更新构建并扩展跨平台支持。它为轻量级工具提供文本界面，吸引复古爱好者和系统开发者。该项目显示出传统工具在不放弃根基的前提下也能持续进化。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cauchy221/Alignment-Whack-a-Mole-Code" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Alignment whack-a-mole: Finetuning activates recall of copyrighted books in LLMs</span><span class='zh'>对齐打地鼠：微调会让 LLM 记忆受版权保护书籍</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A &#x27;alignment whack-a-mole&#x27; project shows finetuning can cause LLMs to recall copyrighted book passages, highlighting risks of data memorization in trained models.</span><span class='zh'>一个名为对齐打地鼠的研究显示，微调可能让大语言模型记忆并召回受版权保护的书籍段落，凸显训练数据记忆带来的版权与安全风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A &#x27;alignment whack-a-mole&#x27; project shows finetuning can cause LLMs to recall copyrighted book passages, highlighting risks of data memorization in trained models. The work prompts discussion on safe alignment techniques and copyright compliance.</span><span class='zh'>一个名为对齐打地鼠的研究显示，微调可能让大语言模型记忆并召回受版权保护的书籍段落，凸显训练数据记忆带来的版权与安全风险。该工作引发关于如何在对齐中降低记忆、并确保版权合规的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/maaslalani/sheets" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sheets: Terminal based spreadsheet tool</span><span class='zh'>Sheets：基于终端的电子表格工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sheets is a terminal-based spreadsheet tool offering quick data manipulation from the command line, with a focus on minimalism and scripting-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Sheets 是一个基于终端的电子表格工具，强调简洁性和可脚本化的工作流，方便在命令行中快速处理数据。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sheets is a terminal-based spreadsheet tool offering quick data manipulation from the command line, with a focus on minimalism and scripting-friendly workflows. It appeals to developers and sysadmins who want fast, keyboard-driven data work without GUI overhead. It highlights a growing niche of CLI-first productivity tools.</span><span class='zh'>Sheets 是一个基于终端的电子表格工具，强调简洁性和可脚本化的工作流，方便在命令行中快速处理数据。它受开发者与系统管理员欢迎，追求无 GUI 的高效键盘操作。凸显了日益兴起的 CLI 优先生产力工具领域。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/brightbeanxyz/brightbean-studio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a social media management tool in 3 weeks with Claude and Codex</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：用 Claude 与 Codex 在3周内打造社媒管理工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Show HN project that builds a social media management tool in three weeks with AI copilots Claude and Codex, illustrating rapid prototyping and the tradeoffs of AI-assisted development.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了用 Claude 与 Codex 在三周内开发出一个社媒管理工具的过程，体现了 AI 辅助快速迭代的潜力与取舍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN project that builds a social media management tool in three weeks with AI copilots Claude and Codex, illustrating rapid prototyping and the tradeoffs of AI-assisted development. It highlights how LLMs can accelerate UI, scheduling, and automation tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了用 Claude 与 Codex 在三周内开发出一个社媒管理工具的过程，体现了 AI 辅助快速迭代的潜力与取舍。项目揭示了在前端、后端和自动化任务中，AI 对工作流程的加速作用。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>RedSun demonstrates a privilege-escalation path that could grant system-level access on Windows 11/10 and Windows Server after the April 2026 update.</span><span class='zh'>RedSun 展示了一条可能在 2026 年 4 月更新后让系统级权限获得的提权路径，影响 Windows 11/10 与 Windows Server。</span></p>

The project highlights potential security gaps and suggests mitigations for defenders.</span><span class='zh'>RedSun 展示了一条可能在 2026 年 4 月更新后让系统级权限获得的提权路径，影响 Windows 11/10 与 Windows Server。该项目暴露了潜在的安全缺口，并为防守端提供了缓解思路。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anzellai/sky" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sky – an Elm-inspired language that compiles to Go</span><span class='zh'>Sky：Elm 启发的语言，编译为 Go</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sky is an Elm-inspired language that compiles to Go, aiming to bring safer, more ergonomic functional style to Go projects.</span><span class='zh'>Sky 是一门受 Elm 启发、可编译为 Go 的语言，旨在为 Go 项目带来更安全、更高效的函数式风格。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sky is an Elm-inspired language that compiles to Go, aiming to bring safer, more ergonomic functional style to Go projects. By targeting Go’s ecosystem, it seeks practical interoperability and smoother state management while offering cleaner abstractions for UI and logic. It represents a thoughtful experiment at the intersection of FP ergonomics and Go&#x27;s pragmatism.</span><span class='zh'>Sky 是一门受 Elm 启发、可编译为 Go 的语言，旨在为 Go 项目带来更安全、更高效的函数式风格。通过面向 Go 生态系统，它寻求实际的互操作性和更流畅的状态管理，同时提供更清晰的 UI 与逻辑抽象。它展示了在函数式编程的易用性与 Go 的务实之间的一个有趣实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/citguru/openduck" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Distributed DuckDB Instance</span><span class='zh'>分布式 DuckDB 实例</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A distributed DuckDB instance aims to run analytical queries across multiple nodes, enabling larger-scale analytics than a single process.</span><span class='zh'>提出在多节点上运行 DuckDB 的分布式实例，以并行化分析查询、突破单进程的容量限制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A distributed DuckDB instance aims to run analytical queries across multiple nodes, enabling larger-scale analytics than a single process. This direction tackles data distribution, parallel execution, and fault tolerance, signaling scalable analytics for analytics workloads.</span><span class='zh'>提出在多节点上运行 DuckDB 的分布式实例，以并行化分析查询、突破单进程的容量限制。该方向需解决数据分布、并行执行和故障恢复等挑战，代表分析型工作负载的可扩展性探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/MartinGalway/C64_music" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Martin Galway&#x27;s music source files from 1980&#x27;s Commodore 64 games</span><span class='zh'>Martin Galway 的1980年代 Commodore 64 遊戲音樂源檔</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Archive release of Martin Galway&#x27;s music source files from 1980s Commodore 64 games.</span><span class='zh'>公開了 Martin Galway 在1980年代 Commodore 64 遊戲的音樂源檔，提供珍貴的早期像素音樂創作見解。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Archive release of Martin Galway&#x27;s music source files from 1980s Commodore 64 games. The materials offer a rare glimpse into early chiptune composition, revealing how Galway layered simple waveforms and timing tricks to craft catchy tunes within severe hardware constraints. For vintage-game enthusiasts and reproducible retro sound design, it&#x27;s a goldmine.</span><span class='zh'>公開了 Martin Galway 在1980年代 Commodore 64 遊戲的音樂源檔，提供珍貴的早期像素音樂創作見解。這些檔案揭示了在有限硬體下，如何運用簡單波形與節奏技巧創作出上癮的音樂。對復古遊戲音效與重現者而言，這是寶貴資源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aloshdenny/reverse-SynthID" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering Gemini&#x27;s SynthID detection</span><span class='zh'>逆向工程 Gemini 的 SynthID 检测</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering Gemini&#x27;s SynthID detection reveals what the system checks to verify authenticity, highlighting the fragility and design trade-offs of hardware-backed identity.</span><span class='zh'>对 Gemini 的 SynthID 检测进行逆向工程，揭示系统为验证真实身份而检查的要点，凸显硬件背书身份的脆弱性与设计权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Reverse engineering Gemini&#x27;s SynthID detection reveals what the system checks to verify authenticity, highlighting the fragility and design trade-offs of hardware-backed identity. The write-up underscores challenges in reliably detecting synthetic IDs and what this implies for future security tooling.</span><span class='zh'>对 Gemini 的 SynthID 检测进行逆向工程，揭示系统为验证真实身份而检查的要点，凸显硬件背书身份的脆弱性与设计权衡。该工作强调在可靠检测合成身份方面的挑战，以及对未来安全工具的启示。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/solod-dev/solod" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Solod – A subset of Go that translates to C</span><span class='zh'>Solod：把 Go 的子集转译成 C 的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Solod is a subset of Go that compiles down to C, enabling tight interop and potential performance benefits without a full Go toolchain.</span><span class='zh'>Solod 是一种 Go 的子集，能够编译成 C，从而在无需完整 Go 工具链的情况下实现高效的互操作性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Solod is a subset of Go that compiles down to C, enabling tight interop and potential performance benefits without a full Go toolchain. The project aims to let developers write familiar Go-like code and target C for ecosystems where C is preferred or required.</span><span class='zh'>Solod 是一种 Go 的子集，能够编译成 C，从而在无需完整 Go 工具链的情况下实现高效的互操作性。该项目让开发者用类似 Go 的语法编写代码，并以 C 作为目标，适用于需要或偏好 C 的场景。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><a class='sday' href='/hackernews/2026/04/06/top_stories_04062026'><span class='en'>Apr 6</span><span class='zh'>4月6日</span></a><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>166</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47669337' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>41</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47811154" data-id="47811154" data-tags="Programming|Code" data-score="165" data-comments="18" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47811154" data-time="1776463979">

The project hints at potential gains in safety, performance, and standard conformance for developers.</span><span class='zh'>Kefir 公布面向 C17/C23 的编译器，凸显对 C 生态中替代工具链的探索。此项目或在安全性、性能与标准兼容性方面为开发者带来潜在收益。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/pgcentralfoundation/pgrx" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pgrx: Build Postgres Extensions with Rust</span><span class='zh'>Pgrx：用 Rust 构建 Postgres 扩展</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pgrx provides a Rust-based framework for developing PostgreSQL extensions, leveraging Rust’s safety and performance.</span><span class='zh'>Pgrx 提供基于 Rust 的框架来开发 PostgreSQL 扩展，利用 Rust 的安全性与性能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pgrx provides a Rust-based framework for developing PostgreSQL extensions, leveraging Rust’s safety and performance. This lowers the barrier for writing robust, high-performance server extensions.</span><span class='zh'>Pgrx 提供基于 Rust 的框架来开发 PostgreSQL 扩展，利用 Rust 的安全性与性能。它简化了扩展的构建、测试与部署，有望扩大 Rust 在数据库工具链中的应用。为编写高性能、健壮的数据库扩展降低了门槛。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DeepSeek-V4 demonstrates an approach to highly efficient million-token context intelligence, enabling long-context reasoning at scale.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek-V4 展示了在百万级上下文下实现高效推理的思路，通过优化内存和检索能力，支持对大规模文档的理解与长时对话。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DeepSeek-V4 demonstrates an approach to highly efficient million-token context intelligence, enabling long-context reasoning at scale. This advancement could empower enterprise search, AI assistants, and research tools that must reference extensive histories.</span><span class='zh'>DeepSeek-V4 展示了在百万级上下文下实现高效推理的思路，通过优化内存和检索能力，支持对大规模文档的理解与长时对话。此进展可能推动企业检索、AI 助手和需要大量历史信息参考的研究工具的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/randerson112/craft" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a Cargo-like build tool for C/C++</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我打造了一个类似 Cargo 的 C/C++ 构建工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Like Cargo for Rust, this tool aims to manage dependencies, build pipelines, and reproducible builds for C/C++.</span><span class='zh'>此工具模仿 Rust 的 Cargo，尝试为 C/C++ 项目管理依赖、构建流水线与可重复构建。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Like Cargo for Rust, this tool aims to manage dependencies, build pipelines, and reproducible builds for C/C++. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MacMind reimagines a transformer neural network running inside HyperCard on an early Macintosh, a playful demonstration of modern ML concepts in a vintage sandbox.</span><span class='zh'>MacMind 将 Transformer 模型在 1989 年 Macintosh 的 HyperCard 环境中实现，体现了在极端受限条件下实验现代 ML 的趣味性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MacMind reimagines a transformer neural network running inside HyperCard on an early Macintosh, a playful demonstration of modern ML concepts in a vintage sandbox. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Netflix/void-model" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VOID: Video Object and Interaction Deletion</span><span class='zh'>VOID：视频对象与互动删除</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Netflix&#x27;s VOID model enables targeted deletion of objects and interactions within videos, supporting privacy-preserving edits and safer content handling.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix 的 VOID 模型实现对视频中的特定对象和互动进行逐位删除，支持隐私保护的内容编辑与更安全的内容处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Netflix&#x27;s VOID model enables targeted deletion of objects and interactions within videos, supporting privacy-preserving edits and safer content handling. By focusing on object-level removal, it aims to reduce re-encoding overhead and preserve remaining content.</span><span class='zh'>Netflix 的 VOID 模型实现对视频中的特定对象和互动进行逐位删除，支持隐私保护的内容编辑与更安全的内容处理。通过面向对象级别的删除，旨在降低重新编码的开销，同时尽量保留其余部分。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/drasimwagan/mdv" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: MDV – a Markdown superset for docs, dashboards, and slides with data</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: MDV — 用于文档、仪表板和包含数据的幻灯片的 Markdown 超集</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MDV adds data-aware blocks to Markdown to support docs, dashboards, and data-driven slides, making content both readable and runnable.</span><span class='zh'>MDV 将 Markdown 扩展为支持文档、仪表板与数据驱动幻灯片的工具，数据块让文档与展示更紧密结合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MDV adds data-aware blocks to Markdown to support docs, dashboards, and data-driven slides, making content both readable and runnable. It aims to unify authoring and presentation for data-heavy work, with tooling to render outputs as dashboards or slide decks. This could streamline workflows for engineers and data scientists.</span><span class='zh'>MDV 将 Markdown 扩展为支持文档、仪表板与数据驱动幻灯片的工具，数据块让文档与展示更紧密结合。旨在为数据科学家和开发者提供统一的写作-呈现工作流，并能直接渲染为仪表板或幻灯片。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/gizmo64k/soulplayer-c64" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Soul Player C64 – A real transformer running on a 1 MHz Commodore 64</span><span class='zh'>Soul Player C64：在1MHz Commodore 64上运行的真实 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A retro AI project demonstrates a Transformer model running on a 1 MHz Commodore 64, pushing the boundaries of what’s possible on vintage hardware.</span><span class='zh'>一项复古 AI 项目在 1MHz 的 Commodore 64 上实现了 Transformer 模型，挑战了这类老旧硬件的边界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A retro AI project demonstrates a Transformer model running on a 1 MHz Commodore 64, pushing the boundaries of what’s possible on vintage hardware. The effort highlights optimization tricks, memory management, and low-power inference strategies that make AI feasible on extreme constraints. It’s a playful yet instructive reminder that edge AI can start with hardware you already own.</span><span class='zh'>一项复古 AI 项目在 1MHz 的 Commodore 64 上实现了 Transformer 模型，挑战了这类老旧硬件的边界。该工作展示了内存管理、模型优化和低功耗推理等技巧，使 AI 推理在极限约束下成为可能。这个案例既有趣又具启发性，提醒我们边缘 AI 的起点可以是手边的旧设备。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NikolayS/pgque" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>PgQue: Zero-Bloat Postgres Queue</span><span class='zh'>PgQue：零臃肿的 PostgreSQL 队列</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introducing PgQue, a lightweight PostgreSQL-backed queue with a tiny footprint designed for reliable background processing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 PgQue，一种极简且零膨胀的 PostgreSQL 队列实现，专注于可靠的后台处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introducing PgQue, a lightweight PostgreSQL-backed queue with a tiny footprint designed for reliable background processing. Emphasizes simplicity, ease of use, and predictable performance for apps needing queueing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 PgQue，一种极简且零膨胀的 PostgreSQL 队列实现，专注于可靠的后台处理。强调简洁性、易用性和可预测的性能，适合需要排队能力的应用场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jkool702/forkrun" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Forkrun – NUMA-aware shell parallelizer (50×–400× faster than parallel)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Forkrun — NUMA感知的Shell并行化器（比 parallel 快50–400倍）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Forkrun introduces NUMA-aware parallelization for shell commands, delivering dramatic speedups on multi-socket servers.</span><span class='zh'>Forkrun 提供对 shell 命令的 NUMA 感知并行化，在多插槽服务器上实现显著的速度提升。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Forkrun introduces NUMA-aware parallelization for shell commands, delivering dramatic speedups on multi-socket servers. The project demonstrates how memory locality and CPU affinity can unlock large performance gains for everyday CLI tasks. It could inspire a new wave of performance-focused shell tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Forkrun 提供对 shell 命令的 NUMA 感知并行化，在多插槽服务器上实现显著的速度提升。该项目展示了内存局部性与 CPU 亲和性如何为日常命令行任务带来巨大的性能提升，或将推动新一轮面向性能的 shell 工具设计。</span></p>
