<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Infisical/agent-vault" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Agent Vault – Open-source credential proxy and vault for agents</span><span class='zh'>Agent Vault – 面向代理的开源凭据代理与保管库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Agent Vault is an open-source credential proxy and vault designed for agents.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Vault 是一个为代理设计的开源凭据代理与保管库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Agent Vault is an open-source credential proxy and vault designed for agents. It centralizes secret management, rotates credentials, and provides auditing for tool usage, aiming to simplify secure automation workflows. If you operate AI or software agents, this could reduce credential sprawl and improve security posture.</span><span class='zh'>Agent Vault 是一个为代理设计的开源凭据代理与保管库。它集中管理机密、轮换凭证并提供审计，旨在简化安全自动化工作流。对于运行 AI 或软件代理的团队，可以减少凭证碎片并提升安全性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Xilem is an experimental Rust-native UI framework targeting high performance and clean abstractions with native widgets and minimal runtime overhead.</span><span class='zh'>Xilem 是一个实验性的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，致力于提供高性能、简洁的抽象和原生控件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Xilem is an experimental Rust-native UI framework targeting high performance and clean abstractions with native widgets and minimal runtime overhead. It appeals to developers seeking efficient, low-level UI tech in Rust.</span><span class='zh'>Xilem 是一个实验性的 Rust 原生 UI 框架，致力于提供高性能、简洁的抽象和原生控件。项目强调最小运行时开销和跨平台潜力，吸引对本地界面开发感兴趣的开发者。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nakagami/grdpwasm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A web-based RDP client built with Go WebAssembly and grdp</span><span class='zh'>基于 Go WebAssembly 和 grdp 的网页 RDP 客户端</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Demonstrates a web-based RDP client built with Go WebAssembly and the grdp library, enabling remote desktop access directly in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>展示一個以 Go WebAssembly 與 grdp 驅動的網頁 RDP 客戶端，實現在瀏覽器中進行遠端桌面連線的可能性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Demonstrates a web-based RDP client built with Go WebAssembly and the grdp library, enabling remote desktop access directly in the browser. The project illustrates how WASM can bring near-native UI responsiveness and performance to browser-based remote sessions, with considerations for security and user experience.</span><span class='zh'>展示一個以 Go WebAssembly 與 grdp 驅動的網頁 RDP 客戶端，實現在瀏覽器中進行遠端桌面連線的可能性。這類實作顯示 WASM 在網頁端的性能與回應性，並需關注安全性與使用者體驗的取捨。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/browser-use/browser-harness" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Browser Harness – Gives LLM freedom to complete any browser task</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Browser Harness 让 LLM 自由完成任意浏览器任务</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Browser Harness provides a framework for LLMs to control a browser directly, enabling a wide range of automated tasks without brittle wrappers.</span><span class='zh'>Browser Harness 提供一个直接让 LLM 控制浏览器的框架，使自动化任务覆盖面更广且不易出错。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Browser Harness provides a framework for LLMs to control a browser directly, enabling a wide range of automated tasks without brittle wrappers. It lowers friction for building end-to-end browser automation.</span><span class='zh'>Browser Harness 提供一个直接让 LLM 控制浏览器的框架，使自动化任务覆盖面更广且不易出错。降低了构建端到端浏览器自动化的门槛。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kitfunso/hippo-memory" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hippo, biologically inspired memory for AI agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hippo——生物启发的AI代理记忆</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hippo proposes memory structures for AI agents inspired by biological systems, aiming to improve long-horizon reasoning and continual learning.</span><span class='zh'>Hippo 提出受生物系统启发的 AI 代理记忆结构，目标是提升长期推理和持续学习的能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hippo proposes memory structures for AI agents inspired by biological systems, aiming to improve long-horizon reasoning and continual learning. By borrowing from how real brains store and retrieve information, it may offer more scalable and robust agent cognition. If you&#x27;re building persistent, capable agents, this approach is worth tracking.</span><span class='zh'>Hippo 提出受生物系统启发的 AI 代理记忆结构，目标是提升长期推理和持续学习的能力。借鉴真实大脑的信息存储与检索机制，或许能带来更可扩展、更稳健的代理认知。若在构建具持久能力的智能代理，值得关注。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rocky-data/rocky" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Rocky – Rust SQL engine with branches, replay, column lineage</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Rocky——具分支、回放与列级血缘的 Rust SQL 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rocky is a Rust-based SQL engine with support for branches, query replay, and column lineage.</span><span class='zh'>Rocky 是一个用 Rust 编写的 SQL 引擎，支持分支、查询回放和列级血缘。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rocky is a Rust-based SQL engine with support for branches, query replay, and column lineage. It aims to be embeddable and auditable, enabling reproducible results and fine-grained data provenance for data pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>Rocky 是一个用 Rust 编写的 SQL 引擎，支持分支、查询回放和列级血缘。目标是可嵌入、可审计，适合需要可重复结果和细粒度数据血缘的数据管线场景。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/kdave/btrfs-progs/issues/1107" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Case study: recovery of a corrupted 12 TB multi-device pool</span><span class='zh'>案例研究：从损坏的 12 TB 多设备池中恢复数据</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A case study on recovering a corrupted 12 TB multi-device pool details salvage steps, including data recovery, metadata repair, and minimizing further damage.</span><span class='zh'>案例研究记录了从损坏的 12 TB 多设备池中恢复数据的过程，涵盖数据挽救、元数据修复及降低进一步损坏的做法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A case study on recovering a corrupted 12 TB multi-device pool details salvage steps, including data recovery, metadata repair, and minimizing further damage. It underscores the importance of backups and careful recovery procedures for multi-disk arrays. Practical guidance for disaster recovery and data integrity.</span><span class='zh'>案例研究记录了从损坏的 12 TB 多设备池中恢复数据的过程，涵盖数据挽救、元数据修复及降低进一步损坏的做法。强调了备份与谨慎恢复在多盘阵列中的重要性，为灾难恢复与数据完整性提供实用要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/NV404/gova" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gova – The declarative GUI framework for Go</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Gova — Go 的声明式 GUI 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gova is a declarative GUI framework for Go that aims to simplify building user interfaces by expressing UI as declarative trees bound to Go data.</span><span class='zh'>Gova 是一个供 Go 使用的声明式 GUI 框架，旨在通过将界面表达为与 Go 数据绑定的声明性树来简化界面开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gova is a declarative GUI framework for Go that aims to simplify building user interfaces by expressing UI as declarative trees bound to Go data. It enables rapid UI development with less boilerplate and a more predictable rendering model.</span><span class='zh'>Gova 是一个供 Go 使用的声明式 GUI 框架，旨在通过将界面表达为与 Go 数据绑定的声明性树来简化界面开发。它提供更少的样板代码和更可预测的渲染模型，提升开发效率。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-deepmind/mujoco" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MuJoCo – Advanced Physics Simulation</span><span class='zh'>MuJoCo：高级物理仿真</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MuJoCo is a high-fidelity physics simulator used in robotics and AI research, enabling fast, accurate dynamics for articulated agents and environments.</span><span class='zh'>MuJoCo 是用于机器人与人工智能研究的高保真物理仿真器，支持对关节式代理和环境的快速、准确动力学计算。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MuJoCo is a high-fidelity physics simulator used in robotics and AI research, enabling fast, accurate dynamics for articulated agents and environments. The project continues to evolve to support more complex models and integration with ML workflows.</span><span class='zh'>MuJoCo 是用于机器人与人工智能研究的高保真物理仿真器，支持对关节式代理和环境的快速、准确动力学计算。该项目持续发展，进一步支持更复杂的模型及与机器学习工作流的整合。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/roscopeco/anos" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Anos – a hand-written ~100KiB microkernel for x86-64 and RISC-V</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Anos——一个手写的 ~100KiB 的微内核，支持 x86-64 与 RISC-V</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Anos is a hand-written microkernel around 100 KiB supporting x86-64 and RISC-V, designed for exploration of OS fundamentals in tiny footprints.</span><span class='zh'>Anos 是一个约 100 KiB 的手写微内核，支持 x86-64 与 RISC-V，用于探索极小体量系统中的操作系统基础。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Anos is a hand-written microkernel around 100 KiB supporting x86-64 and RISC-V, designed for exploration of OS fundamentals in tiny footprints. It serves as a learning and research vehicle for kernel design and cross-architecture ideas. It’s a niche project, but interesting for students and OS enthusiasts.</span><span class='zh'>Anos 是一个约 100 KiB 的手写微内核，支持 x86-64 与 RISC-V，用于探索极小体量系统中的操作系统基础。它可以作为内核设计与跨架构思路的学习与研究载体，对计算机系学生和操作系统爱好者具有吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cmc-labo/tinyos-rtos" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TinyOS – A minimalist RTOS for Cortex-M written in C</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TinyOS — 为 Cortex-M 编写的极简 RTOS</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TinyOS is a minimalist real-time operating system for Cortex-M MCUs written in C, aimed at keeping footprint tiny while preserving essential RTOS features.</span><span class='zh'>TinyOS 提供了一个用于 Cortex-M 微控制器、用 C 编写的极简实时操作系统，强调在保持小内存占用的同时保留关键的 RTOS 特性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TinyOS is a minimalist real-time operating system for Cortex-M MCUs written in C, aimed at keeping footprint tiny while preserving essential RTOS features. It serves as a compact platform for education, experimentation, and small embedded projects where predictability matters. The project embodies the ethos of lean, hardware-conscious OS design.</span><span class='zh'>TinyOS 提供了一个用于 Cortex-M 微控制器、用 C 编写的极简实时操作系统，强调在保持小内存占用的同时保留关键的 RTOS 特性。它适合作为教育、实验和对确定性有高要求的嵌入式小项目的平台。此项目体现了对瘦型、硬件友好操作系统设计的追求。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Show HN post describes piping free sports streams into Jellyfin via an HLS restream proxy, enabling ad-free, self-hosted viewing. It discusses practicality and potential legality of aggregating free streams into a personal media server.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 分享了通过HLS中继代理将免费体育流推送到Jellyfin，实现无广告的自托管观看。文中谈及 practicality 与将免费流聚合到自建媒体服务器的潜在合规性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/libsdl-org/SDL/issues/15350" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SDL bans AI-written commits</span><span class='zh'>SDL 禁止 AI 自动生成的提交</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The SDL project bans commits authored by AI, citing concerns about provenance and reproducibility, pushing contributors to ensure human-generated edits.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 项目禁止 AI 自动生成的提交，基于可追溯性与可重复性等顾虑，要求贡献者确保人为编辑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The SDL project bans commits authored by AI, citing concerns about provenance and reproducibility, pushing contributors to ensure human-generated edits.</span><span class='zh'>SDL 项目禁止 AI 自动生成的提交，基于可追溯性与可重复性等顾虑，要求贡献者确保人为编辑。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EvanFlow uses TDD to drive feedback loop for Claude Code, enabling automated tests and regression checks.</span><span class='zh'>EvanFlow 通过测试驱动开发推动 Claude Code 的反馈循环，支持自动化测试与回归检查。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CodeBurn analyzes Claude Code&#x27;s token usage by task, helping developers understand how compute and cost scale with different inputs.</span><span class='zh'>CodeBurn 按任务分析 Claude 在代码任务中的 token 使用，帮助开发者理解不同输入对算力与成本的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/aallan/vera" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Vera: a programming language designed for machines to write</span><span class='zh'>Vera：为机器编写代码而设计的编程语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Vera is a programming language aimed at machines writing code, exploring how automated generation can shape software development.</span><span class='zh'>Vera 是一门为“让机器去写代码”而设计的编程语言，研究自动化生成如何改变软件开发。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Vera is a programming language aimed at machines writing code, exploring how automated generation can shape software development. Early experiments show potential for synthesis-driven workflows and new tooling paradigms.</span><span class='zh'>Vera 是一门为“让机器去写代码”而设计的编程语言，研究自动化生成如何改变软件开发。早期实验显示在合成驱动的工作流和新工具范式方面的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dropseed/plain" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Plain – The full-stack Python framework designed for humans and agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: Plain——为人和代理人设计的全栈 Python 框架</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Plain is a full-stack Python framework designed for humans and AI agents building apps.</span><span class='zh'>Plain 是一个为人和 AI 代理人打造应用的全栈 Python 框架，强调开发者体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Plain is a full-stack Python framework designed for humans and AI agents building apps. It emphasizes developer ergonomics, offering end-to-end tooling, clear abstractions, and smooth integration with AI workflows to speed up common tasks.</span><span class='zh'>Plain 是一个为人和 AI 代理人打造应用的全栈 Python 框架，强调开发者体验。它提供端到端工具、清晰的抽象以及与 AI 工作流的无缝整合，帮助加速常见任务的实现。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Arc1011/KernelUNO" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ibuilt a tiny Unix‑like &#x27;OS&#x27; with shell and filesystem for Arduino UNO (2KB RAM)</span><span class='zh'>用 Arduino UNO（仅 2KB RAM）构建的微型 Unix‑like 操作系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I built a tiny Unix-like OS with a shell and filesystem for Arduino UNO, constrained to 2KB RAM.</span><span class='zh'>在仅有 2KB RAM 的 Arduino UNO 上构建了一个微型的类 Unix 操作系统，包含 shell 与文件系统。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I built a tiny Unix-like OS with a shell and filesystem for Arduino UNO, constrained to 2KB RAM. It pushes the boundaries of what small microcontrollers can run and demonstrates minimalist OS design. A provocative example for embedded systems enthusiasts.</span><span class='zh'>在仅有 2KB RAM 的 Arduino UNO 上构建了一个微型的类 Unix 操作系统，包含 shell 与文件系统。此举挑战了微控制器的极限，展示了极简操作系统设计的可能性，适合嵌入式系统爱好者参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RyanCodrai/py-turboquant" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TurboQuant for vector search – 2-4 bit compression</span><span class='zh'>向量检索的 TurboQuant：2-4 位压缩</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboQuant for vector search demonstrates 2-4 bit quantization to shrink vector indexes while preserving retrieval quality.</span><span class='zh'>向量检索的 TurboQuant 通过 2-4 位量化来压缩向量索引，同时尽量保持检索质量。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboQuant for vector search demonstrates 2-4 bit quantization to shrink vector indexes while preserving retrieval quality. The project shows practical gains for scalable similarity search, enabling larger datasets and lower memory footprints. It signals a trend toward tighter compression in AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>向量检索的 TurboQuant 通过 2-4 位量化来压缩向量索引，同时尽量保持检索质量。此举为大规模相似性检索带来实际的内存与成本收益，支持更大数据集与更低内存开销。反映了在 AI 工作负载中更紧凑编码的趋势。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/microsoft/sudo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sudo for Windows (2024)</span><span class='zh'>Windows 的 sudo</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft introduces a sudo-like tool for Windows, enabling non-root commands with elevated privileges and unifying developer workflows with Linux environments.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Windows 的 sudo 工具，让在 Windows 上的命令提升权限更加类似 Linux 的 sudo 行为。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft introduces a sudo-like tool for Windows, enabling non-root commands with elevated privileges and unifying developer workflows with Linux environments. It signals a shift toward more Linux-style privilege management on Windows.</span><span class='zh'>微软推出 Windows 的 sudo 工具，让在 Windows 上的命令提升权限更加类似 Linux 的 sudo 行为。该功能有助于统一开发工作流、提升权限管理的一致性，同时提升安全性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/andmarti1424/sc-im" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sc-im: Spreadsheets in your terminal</span><span class='zh'>Sc-im：终端中的电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Sc-im is a terminal-based spreadsheet editor that brings spreadsheet capabilities to the command line, with keyboard-driven navigation.</span><span class='zh'>Sc-im 是一个在终端中运行的电子表格编辑器，支持键盘驱动的操作，直接在命令行里完成数据处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Sc-im is a terminal-based spreadsheet editor that brings spreadsheet capabilities to the command line, with keyboard-driven navigation. It appeals to power users who need quick data manipulation on headless or low-resource environments, without leaving the terminal. For lightweight workflows, it can be a surprisingly productive alternative to GUI spreadsheets.</span><span class='zh'>Sc-im 是一个在终端中运行的电子表格编辑器，支持键盘驱动的操作，直接在命令行里完成数据处理。它特别适合在无头或资源受限的环境下进行快速数据操作的高阶用户，无需离开终端即可完成工作。对于轻量级的工作流，这是一个出人意料的高效替代方案。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rwc9u/emacs-libgterm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Emacs-libgterm: Terminal emulator for Emacs using libghostty-vt</span><span class='zh'>Emacs-libgterm：基于 libghostty-vt 的 Emacs 终端模拟器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Emacs-libgterm brings terminal emulation to Emacs via libghostty-vt, enabling robust interactive shells and terminal apps inside the editor.</span><span class='zh'>Emacs-libgterm 通过 libghostty-vt 将终端仿真引入 Emacs，使编辑器内可使用强大的互动式 shell 与终端应用。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Emacs-libgterm brings terminal emulation to Emacs via libghostty-vt, enabling robust interactive shells and terminal apps inside the editor. This reduces external terminal switches and benefits developers who rely on REPLs or TUI tools.</span><span class='zh'>Emacs-libgterm 通过 libghostty-vt 将终端仿真引入 Emacs，使编辑器内可使用强大的互动式 shell 与终端应用。减少了切换外部终端的需求，提升对 REPL 与文本界面工具的支持。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/duane1024/l123" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>L123: A Lotus 1-2-3–style terminal spreadsheet with modern Excel compatibility</span><span class='zh'>L123：具现代 Excel 兼容性的 Lotus 1-2-3 风格终端电子表格</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>L123 recreates Lotus 1-2-3-style terminal spreadsheets with modern Excel compatibility, offering a lightweight, text-based workflow for quick data entry and retro computing nostalgia.</span><span class='zh'>L123 将 Lotus 1-2-3 风格的终端电子表格与现代 Excel 兼容性结合，提供轻量级、文本界面的快速数据处理体验，兼具复古计算的乐趣。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>L123 recreates Lotus 1-2-3-style terminal spreadsheets with modern Excel compatibility, offering a lightweight, text-based workflow for quick data entry and retro computing nostalgia. It provides a bridge for users who want a fast terminal UI while still leveraging Excel import/export features.</span><span class='zh'>L123 将 Lotus 1-2-3 风格的终端电子表格与现代 Excel 兼容性结合，提供轻量级、文本界面的快速数据处理体验，兼具复古计算的乐趣。它让偏好终端工作流的用户仍然能够使用 Excel 的导入导出功能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/riyaneel/Tachyon/tree/main/docs/adr" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bypassing the kernel for 56ns cross-language IPC</span><span class='zh'>跨语言 IPC 56 纳秒：绕过内核的实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Describes a user-space IPC approach that bypasses the kernel to achieve sub-100ns latency across languages, with implications for high-performance polyglot runtimes and microservices.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一种通过用户空间实现的跨语言 IPC，绕过内核以达到 56 纳秒级别的低延迟，对高性能的多语言运行时和微服务架构具有启示。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Describes a user-space IPC approach that bypasses the kernel to achieve sub-100ns latency across languages, with implications for high-performance polyglot runtimes and microservices.</span><span class='zh'>介绍一种通过用户空间实现的跨语言 IPC，绕过内核以达到 56 纳秒级别的低延迟，对高性能的多语言运行时和微服务架构具有启示。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Python-based WYSIWYG editor demonstrates a usable, lightweight rich-text tool built with a high-level language.</span><span class='zh'>一个用 Python 实现的所见即所得文字处理器展示了轻量、离线友好的富文本编辑能力。</span></p>

It illustrates that productive desktop apps can be built outside mainstream UI stacks, though ecosystem trade-offs apply.</span><span class='zh'>一个用 Python 实现的所见即所得文字处理器展示了轻量、离线友好的富文本编辑能力。证明高层语言也能支撑实用的桌面应用，但在性能和生态方面需要权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/navid-m/chip8emu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I wrote a CHIP-8 emulator in my own programming language</span><span class='zh'>我用自己的编程语言写了一个 CHIP-8 仿真器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A CHIP-8 emulator was implemented using a self-made programming language, illustrating how language design can simplify implementing classic VM targets.</span><span class='zh'>作者用自创的编程语言实现了 CHIP-8 仿真器，展示了语言设计如何让实现简洁直观。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A CHIP-8 emulator was implemented using a self-made programming language, illustrating how language design can simplify implementing classic VM targets. The project demonstrates portability and the educational value of building language ecosystems around small, approachable targets. It offers a tangible example of how language features can map neatly to a simple emulator.</span><span class='zh'>作者用自创的编程语言实现了 CHIP-8 仿真器，展示了语言设计如何让实现简洁直观。此类练手项目有助于理解指令集、虚拟机实现与跨平台移植的要点。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/squadette/rubysyn/blob/master/README.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rubysyn: Clarifying Ruby&#x27;s Syntax and Semantics</span><span class='zh'>Rubysyn：澄清 Ruby 的语法与语义</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rubysyn aims to clarify Ruby&#x27;s syntax and semantics, providing concrete examples and explanations that address common confusion.</span><span class='zh'>Rubysyn 旨在澄清 Ruby 的语法与语义，提供具体示例和解释，帮助开发者理清常见的用法误区。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rubysyn aims to clarify Ruby&#x27;s syntax and semantics, providing concrete examples and explanations that address common confusion. It helps Ruby developers reason about parsing, blocks, and method calls by mapping language rules to practical usage.</span><span class='zh'>Rubysyn 旨在澄清 Ruby 的语法与语义，提供具体示例和解释，帮助开发者理清常见的用法误区。通过将语言规则映射到实际用法，提升对解析、块、方法调用等细节的理解。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/razvandimescu/numa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a DNS resolver from scratch in Rust – no DNS libraries</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：我用 Rust 从零构建了一个 DNS 解析器——不使用任何 DNS 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A hands-on demonstration building a DNS resolver from first principles in Rust, without using existing DNS libraries.</span><span class='zh'>展示了用 Rust 从零实现 DNS 解析器的实作过程，未使用现成 DNS 库。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A hands-on demonstration building a DNS resolver from first principles in Rust, without using existing DNS libraries. The project highlights design decisions, safety considerations, and performance implications of implementing core networking protocols by hand.</span><span class='zh'>展示了用 Rust 从零实现 DNS 解析器的实作过程，未使用现成 DNS 库。该项目揭示核心网络协议的实现要点、安全性考量与性能取舍。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/serverless-dna/tendril" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tendril – a self-extending agent that builds and registers its own tools</span><span class='zh'>Tendril —— 一个自我扩展的代理，能构建并注册自己的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Tendril is a self-extending agent that can build and register its own tools, enabling autonomous expansion of capabilities without separate human setups.</span><span class='zh'>Tendril 是一个能够自我扩展、构建并注册自身工具的代理，能在无需额外人工配置的情况下扩展能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Tendril is a self-extending agent that can build and register its own tools, enabling autonomous expansion of capabilities without separate human setups. The project spotlights the potential—and the governance challenges—of agents that bootstrap their own toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>Tendril 是一个能够自我扩展、构建并注册自身工具的代理，能在无需额外人工配置的情况下扩展能力。该项目凸显了自我引导工具链的潜力与治理挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mwallner/mergetopus" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Git helper tool that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks</span><span class='zh'>将大合并拆分为可并行任务的 Git 助手工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mergetopus is a Git helper that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks, enabling faster integration and more scalable conflict resolution on big repositories.</span><span class='zh'>Mergetopus 将大型 Git 合并拆分为可并行的任务，从而在大型代码库中实现更快的合并、以及更具扩展性的冲突解决。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mergetopus is a Git helper that breaks large merges into parallelizable tasks, enabling faster integration and more scalable conflict resolution on big repositories. It distributes work across cores or workers to boost throughput during merges.</span><span class='zh'>Mergetopus 将大型 Git 合并拆分为可并行的任务，从而在大型代码库中实现更快的合并、以及更具扩展性的冲突解决。它将工作分发到多核或多线程以提升吞吐量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/nyigoro/lumina-lang" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Lumina – a statically typed web-native language for JavaScript and WASM</span><span class='zh'>Lumina：面向 JavaScript 与 WASM 的静态类型化网页原生语言</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Lumina is a statically typed web-native language designed for web targets, interoperating with JavaScript and WebAssembly.</span><span class='zh'>Lumina 是一门面向网页原生的静态类型语言，设计用于与 JavaScript 和 WebAssembly 的互操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Lumina is a statically typed web-native language designed for web targets, interoperating with JavaScript and WebAssembly. The language aims to improve safety and performance for web apps while keeping developer ergonomics.</span><span class='zh'>Lumina 是一门面向网页原生的静态类型语言，设计用于与 JavaScript 和 WebAssembly 的互操作。它旨在在提升网络应用安全性与性能的同时，保持开发者的使用体验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/esutcu/planb-lpm" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A cache-friendly IPv6 LPM with AVX-512 (linearized B+-tree, real BGP benchmarks)</span><span class='zh'>基于 AVX-512 的缓存友好 IPv6 LPM（线性化 B+ 树，真实 BGP 基准测试）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A cache-friendly IPv6 LPM designed around AVX-512 uses a linearized B+-tree to boost route lookups under real BGP workloads.</span><span class='zh'>基于 AVX-512 的缓存友好 IPv6 LPM 通过线性化 B+ 树提升实际 BGP 工作负载下的路由查找速度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A cache-friendly IPv6 LPM designed around AVX-512 uses a linearized B+-tree to boost route lookups under real BGP workloads. Benchmarks show meaningful improvements for high-speed routers and network software, with implications for future data-plane design.</span><span class='zh'>基于 AVX-512 的缓存友好 IPv6 LPM 通过线性化 B+ 树提升实际 BGP 工作负载下的路由查找速度。基准测试显示对高带宽路由器和网络软件有显著提升，对未来数据平面设计具有潜在影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Yus314/kasane" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Kasane: New drop-in Kakoune front end with GPU rendering and WASM Plugins</span><span class='zh'>Kasane：新一代 Kakoune 前端，支持 GPU 渲染与 WASM 插件</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Kasane is a drop-in Kakoune front end with GPU rendering and WASM plugins, aiming to speed up editing and enable extensibility.</span><span class='zh'>Kasane 是一个可直接替换 Kakoune 前端的实现，支持 GPU 渲染和 WASM 插件，旨在提升编辑性能与扩展性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Kasane is a drop-in Kakoune front end with GPU rendering and WASM plugins, aiming to speed up editing and enable extensibility. The project blends hardware acceleration with a modern plugin model to enhance performance and customization. It signals growing interest in high-performance editors.</span><span class='zh'>Kasane 是一个可直接替换 Kakoune 前端的实现，支持 GPU 渲染和 WASM 插件，旨在提升编辑性能与扩展性。该项目将硬件加速与现代插件模型结合，提升了性能与可定制性，体现了对高性能文本编辑器的关注增多。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/4RH1T3CT0R7/ttf-doom" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TTF-DOOM – A raycaster running inside TrueType font hinting</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：TTF-DOOM——在 TrueType 字体提示处理中运行的光线投射渲染器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TTF-DOOM is a playful experiment that runs a raycaster inside TrueType font hinting, turning font hinting into a tiny compute sandbox for visuals.</span><span class='zh'>TTF-DOOM 将光线投射渲染器嵌入到 TrueType 字体提示处理中，利用字体 hinting 进行小规模计算与渲染的实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TTF-DOOM is a playful experiment that runs a raycaster inside TrueType font hinting, turning font hinting into a tiny compute sandbox for visuals. It showcases creative use of typography pipelines and could inspire niche demonstrations of how fonts can carry computational ideas. For enthusiasts, it’s a proof-of-concept bridging fonts and retro rendering.</span><span class='zh'>TTF-DOOM 将光线投射渲染器嵌入到 TrueType 字体提示处理中，利用字体 hinting 进行小规模计算与渲染的实验。展示了排版管线的创造性用法，可能启发将字体与计算概念结合的有趣演示。对爱好者来说，这是字体与光线投射的概念验证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Pathos0925/SatoshiGuesser" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SatoshiGuesser – Roll for Bitcoin</span><span class='zh'>SatoshiGuesser – 为比特币来一把的掷骰子游戏</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A GitHub project that gamifies Bitcoin odds by rolling dice to simulate outcomes related to Satoshi, the smallest unit of BTC.</span><span class='zh'>一个 GitHub 项目，通过掷骰子来模拟与比特币最小单位聪相关的赔率，玩味十足。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A GitHub project that gamifies Bitcoin odds by rolling dice to simulate outcomes related to Satoshi, the smallest unit of BTC. The project blends crypto enthusiasm with probability play and can serve as a playful educational tool for understanding randomness in crypto systems.</span><span class='zh'>一个 GitHub 项目，通过掷骰子来模拟与比特币最小单位聪相关的赔率，玩味十足。该项目将对加密货币的热情与概率游戏结合，是理解区块链系统中随机性的有趣教育工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/Sidenai/sidex" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>SideX – A Tauri-based port of Visual Studio Code</span><span class='zh'>SideX：一个基于 Tauri 的 Visual Studio Code 端口</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SideX is a Visual Studio Code-like editor built on Tauri, offering a native-like VS Code experience with a lighter footprint and improved security isolation.</span><span class='zh'>SideX 是一个基于 Tauri 的 Visual Studio Code 风格编辑器，提供接近原生的体验，同时降低资源占用并提升安全隔离。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SideX is a Visual Studio Code-like editor built on Tauri, offering a native-like VS Code experience with a lighter footprint and improved security isolation. By leveraging Rust and a minimal runtime, it avoids Electron bloat while maintaining familiar workflows. This matters for developers who want a self-contained editor without heavy dependencies.</span><span class='zh'>SideX 是一个基于 Tauri 的 Visual Studio Code 风格编辑器，提供接近原生的体验，同时降低资源占用并提升安全隔离。通过使用 Rust 与更小的运行时，它摆脱了 Electron 的臃肿并保持熟悉的工作流。对于想要无需大量依赖的自包含编辑器的开发者，这很有吸引力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/RokoMijic/MercurialDyson/blob/main/written_report.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mercurial Dyson – a plan for the disassembly of planet Mercury</span><span class='zh'>水星的 Mercurial Dyson 计划：拆解行星以获取能源</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A speculative concept proposes disassembling Mercury and reassembling its mass into energy-harvesting structures, a thought experiment in megastructures and planetary engineering.</span><span class='zh'>一个名为 Mercurial Dyson 的设想提出拆解水星并将物质量转化为能源载荷的 megastructure 思辨，探索行星级工程的能量规模与可行性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A speculative concept proposes disassembling Mercury and reassembling its mass into energy-harvesting structures, a thought experiment in megastructures and planetary engineering. The piece uses Mercury as a lens to explore energy scales, feasibility, and the physics of colossal projects. It provokes reflection on the bounds of future energy ambitions.</span><span class='zh'>一个名为 Mercurial Dyson 的设想提出拆解水星并将物质量转化为能源载荷的 megastructure 思辨，探索行星级工程的能量规模与可行性。通过水星这一案例，讨论巨大工程的物理边界与未来能源野心的潜在局限。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://codeberg.org/aenertia/foxing" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Foxing aspires to be an eBPF-powered replication engine for Linux filesystems</span><span class='zh'>Foxing 旨在成为一个基于 eBPF 的 Linux 文件系统复制引擎</span></a><span class="dom">codeberg.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Foxing aims to use eBPF to track and replicate filesystem changes efficiently, enabling near real-time replication and advanced backup workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Foxing 打算利用 eBPF 追踪并高效复制文件系统变更，支持近实时复制与高级备份工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Foxing aims to use eBPF to track and replicate filesystem changes efficiently, enabling near real-time replication and advanced backup workflows. The project could reshape how backups, snapshots, and disaster recovery work on Linux, but it must prove stability and safety in kernel-level tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Foxing 打算利用 eBPF 追踪并高效复制文件系统变更，支持近实时复制与高级备份工作流。该项目可能改变 Linux 备份、快照与灾难恢复的实现方式，但在内核级工具的稳定性与安全性方面还需验证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/oven-sh/bun/pull/28801" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bun: cgroup-aware AvailableParallelism / HardwareConcurrency on Linux</span><span class='zh'>Bun：在 Linux 上实现对 cgroup 的 AvailableParallelism/HWConcurrency 的感知</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Bun pull request makes AvailableParallelism and HardwareConcurrency aware of Linux cgroups, enabling the runtime to respect container resource limits and avoid oversubscribing CPUs.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 的一个 PR 让 AvailableParallelism 与 HardwareConcurrency 能感知 Linux cgroups，确保运行时遵守容器资源限制，避免 CPU 过载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Bun pull request makes AvailableParallelism and HardwareConcurrency aware of Linux cgroups, enabling the runtime to respect container resource limits and avoid oversubscribing CPUs. The takeaway is better performance isolation for multi-tenant workloads.</span><span class='zh'>Bun 的一个 PR 让 AvailableParallelism 与 HardwareConcurrency 能感知 Linux cgroups，确保运行时遵守容器资源限制，避免 CPU 过载。对于多租户场景性能隔离更友好。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SolveSpace, an open-source CAD tool, reportedly runs on Windows 2000 in 2025, showing remarkable legacy-support and community effort.</span><span class='zh'>开源的 SolveSpace CAD 工具在 2025 年仍能在 Windows 2000 上运行，展示了社区对向后兼容的坚持与努力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SolveSpace, an open-source CAD tool, reportedly runs on Windows 2000 in 2025, showing remarkable legacy-support and community effort. This raises considerations for backward compatibility, developer maintenance, and the longevity of open-source software.</span><span class='zh'>开源的 SolveSpace CAD 工具在 2025 年仍能在 Windows 2000 上运行，展示了社区对向后兼容的坚持与努力。这引发了对向后兼容、维护工作量及开源软件寿命的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/carlovalenti/TRiP" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: TRiP – a complete transformer engine in C built from scratch just by me</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: TRiP — 我用 C 从零实现的完整 Transformer 引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TRiP is a complete transformer engine implemented in C from scratch by a single developer.</span><span class='zh'>TRiP 是一个由单人用 C 语言从零实现的完整 Transformer 引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TRiP is a complete transformer engine implemented in C from scratch by a single developer. The project demonstrates high-performance, low-level optimization for neural network inference and offers insights into architecture decisions, memory layout, and SIMD usage. It&#x27;s a rare solo attempt at building an end-to-end inference stack, valuable for systems programmers and ML researchers alike.</span><span class='zh'>TRiP 是一个由单人用 C 语言从零实现的完整 Transformer 引擎。项目展示了面向神经网络推理的高性能、低层优化，揭示了架构设计、内存布局和 SIMD 的使用等要点，对于系统程序员和机器学习研究者都具有参考价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/google-labs-code/design.md" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Design.md: A format spec for describing a visual identity to coding agents</span><span class='zh'>Design.md：一种将视觉识别描述给编码代理的格式规范</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Design.md proposes a machine-readable format to describe a brand&#x27;s visual identity for coding agents and AI systems.</span><span class='zh'>Design.md 提出一种机器可读的格式，用于向编码代理描述品牌的视觉识别要素。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Python API to control backlit keyboards opens up programmable lighting for productivity and accessibility, enabling dynamic visual cues or aesthetic customization within Python apps.</span><span class='zh'>一个用于控制背光键盘的 Python API，为生产力与无障碍提供可编程灯效，允许在 Python 应用中实现动态视觉提示或外观自定义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shreeve/rip-lang" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Rip language. Compiles to ES2022. Built-in reactivity</span><span class='zh'>Rip 语言：编译到 ES2022，内置响应式特性</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rip is a new language that compiles to ES2022 with built-in reactivity, aiming to simplify building reactive web apps.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Rip 语言——一门将目标编译为 ES2022、内置响应式特性的全新语言，旨在简化构建响应式网页应用的工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rip is a new language that compiles to ES2022 with built-in reactivity, aiming to simplify building reactive web apps. The design focuses on ergonomic syntax and frontend performance for modern developers.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Rip 语言——一门将目标编译为 ES2022、内置响应式特性的全新语言，旨在简化构建响应式网页应用的工作。设计重点在于提升前端开发者的编码体验与性能。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/SethPyle376/hiraeth" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Hiraeth – AWS Emulator</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Hiraeth — AWS 仿真器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hiraeth is an AWS emulator for local development, letting developers simulate AWS services offline with a reproducible environment.</span><span class='zh'>Hiraeth 是一个用于本地开发的 AWS 仿真器，允许开发者在离线环境中模拟 AWS 服务，并实现可重复的开发流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hiraeth is an AWS emulator for local development, letting developers simulate AWS services offline with a reproducible environment.</span><span class='zh'>Hiraeth 是一个用于本地开发的 AWS 仿真器，允许开发者在离线环境中模拟 AWS 服务，并实现可重复的开发流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/rockcat/HATS" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI agents that argue with each other to improve decisions</span><span class='zh'>互相辩论的AI代理以提升决策</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI agents that argue with each other can surface better explanations and catch errors, pushing decisions toward higher quality outcomes.</span><span class='zh'>让 AI 代理相互辩论以改进决策的思路显示出提高解释力与发现错误的潜力，可能成为支持决策的有力工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI agents that argue with each other can surface better explanations and catch errors, pushing decisions toward higher quality outcomes. The approach shows promise for decision support, though it raises calibration and safety questions.</span><span class='zh'>让 AI 代理相互辩论以改进决策的思路显示出提高解释力与发现错误的潜力，可能成为支持决策的有力工具。此方法前景乐观，但也需关注校准和安全性等挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/yapstudios/sfsym" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Sfsym – Export Apple SF Symbols as Vector SVG/PDF/PNG</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Sfsym 将 Apple SF Symbols 导出为矢量格式</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Introduces Sfsym, a tool that exports Apple SF Symbols to scalable vector formats (SVG, PDF) or PNG for design workflows.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Sfsym，这是一个将 Apple 的 SF Symbols 导出为可缩放矢量格式（SVG、PDF）或 PNG 的工具，方便设计工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Introduces Sfsym, a tool that exports Apple SF Symbols to scalable vector formats (SVG, PDF) or PNG for design workflows. Enables designers to integrate system icons into custom UI assets without manual tracing.</span><span class='zh'>介绍 Sfsym，这是一个将 Apple 的 SF Symbols 导出为可缩放矢量格式（SVG、PDF）或 PNG 的工具，方便设计工作流。让设计师能够在自定义 UI 资源中直接使用系统图标，而无需手动追踪。</span></p>
